import {
  BrowserRouter,
  Navigate,
  Route,
  Routes,
} from "react-router-dom";

import { AuthProvider } from "./context/AuthContext";
import ProtectedRoute from "./components/ProtectedRoute";

import HomePage from "./pages/public/HomePage";
import AboutPage from "./pages/public/AboutPage";
import LoginPage from "./pages/public/LoginPage";
import RegisterPage from "./pages/public/RegisterPage";

import StudentLayout from "./layouts/StudentLayout";

import AssessmentPage from "./pages/student/AssessmentPage";
import StudentDashboardPage from "./pages/student/StudentDashboardPage";
import CoursesPage from "./pages/student/CoursesPage";
import CourseLearningPage from "./pages/student/CourseLearningPage";
import CourseTestPage from "./pages/student/CourseTestPage";
import CertificatesPage from "./pages/student/CertificatesPage";
import SimulationPage from "./pages/student/SimulationPage";

import { SimulationsPage } from "./pages/student/StudentPlaceholders";
import SettingsPage from "./pages/student/SettingsPage";
import AdminDashboardPage from "./pages/admin/AdminDashboardPage";

function AdminPlaceholder() {
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 p-10 text-white">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-black">
        Área Administrativa
      </h1>

      <p className="mt-2 text-slate-500">
        Gestão administrativa do SICSI III.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <BrowserRouter>
      <AuthProvider>
        <Routes>
          <Route
            path="/"
            element={<HomePage />}
          />

          <Route
            path="/sobre"
            element={<AboutPage />}
          />

          <Route
            path="/login"
            element={<LoginPage />}
          />

          <Route
            path="/registo"
            element={<RegisterPage />}
          />

          <Route
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute role="student" />
            }
          >
            <Route
              element={<StudentLayout />}
            >
              <Route
                path="/student"
                element={
                  <StudentDashboardPage />
                }
              />

              <Route
                path="/student/cursos"
                element={<CoursesPage />}
              />

              <Route
                path="/student/cursos/:courseId"
                element={
                  <CourseLearningPage />
                }
              />

              <Route
                path="/student/cursos/:courseId/teste"
                element={
                  <CourseTestPage />
                }
              />

              <Route
                path="/student/simulacoes"
                element={
                  <SimulationsPage />
                }
              />

              <Route
                path="/student/simulacoes/:simulationId"
                element={
                  <SimulationPage />
                }
              />

              <Route
                path="/student/certificados"
                element={
                  <CertificatesPage />
                }
              />

              <Route
                path="/student/definicoes"
                element={
                  <SettingsPage />
                }
              />
            </Route>
          </Route>

          <Route
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute role="student" />
            }
          >
            <Route
              path="/student/autoavaliacao"
              element={
                <AssessmentPage />
              }
            />
          </Route>

          <Route
            element={
              <ProtectedRoute role="admin" />
            }
          >
            <Route
              path="/admin/*"
              element={<AdminDashboardPage />}
            />
          </Route>

          <Route
            path="*"
            element={
              <Navigate
                to="/"
                replace
              />
            }
          />
        </Routes>
      </AuthProvider>
    </BrowserRouter>
  );
}

import { AlertTriangle, X } from "lucide-react";

export default function ConfirmDialog({
  open,
  title = "Confirmar ação",
  message = "Tem a certeza de que deseja continuar?",
  confirmLabel = "Confirmar",
  cancelLabel = "Cancelar",
  onConfirm,
  onCancel,
  destructive = false,
}) {
  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/80 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm">
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        className="w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-slate-700/80 bg-slate-900 shadow-2xl shadow-black/40"
      >
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-800 px-6 py-5">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
            <div
              className={[
                "flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl",
                destructive
                  ? "bg-red-500/10 text-red-400"
                  : "bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400",
              ].join(" ")}
            >
              <AlertTriangle size={21} />
            </div>

            <div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white">
                {title}
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
                {message}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onCancel}
            className="rounded-xl p-2 text-slate-500 transition hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-white"
            aria-label="Fechar"
          >
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 px-6 py-5 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onCancel}
            className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-300 transition hover:border-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-white"
          >
            {cancelLabel}
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onConfirm}
            className={[
              "rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition",
              destructive
                ? "bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500"
                : "bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500",
            ].join(" ")}
          >
            {confirmLabel}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\components\ProtectedRoute.jsx
==========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";

export default function ProtectedRoute({ role }) {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();

  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    setChecking(false);
  }, [location.pathname]);

  if (loading || checking) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 flex items-center justify-center text-white">
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="mx-auto mb-4 h-10 w-10 animate-spin rounded-full border-4 border-blue-500 border-t-transparent" />
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">A carregar SICSI...</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!user) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace state={{ from: location }} />;
  }

  if (role && user.role !== role) {
    return (
      <Navigate
        to={user.role === "admin" ? "/admin" : "/student"}
        replace
      />
    );
  }

  if (
    user.role === "student" &&
    !user.initial_assessment_completed &&
    location.pathname !== "/student/autoavaliacao"
  ) {
    return <Navigate to="/student/autoavaliacao" replace />;
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}


==========================================================================================
FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\context\AuthContext.jsx
==========================================================================================
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from "react";

const AuthContext = createContext(null);

const STORAGE_KEY = "sicsi_user";

function readStoredUser() {
  try {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);

    if (!stored) {
      return null;
    }

    return JSON.parse(stored);
  } catch {
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    return null;
  }
}

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    const storedUser = readStoredUser();

    if (storedUser) {
      setUser(storedUser);
    }

    setLoading(false);
  }, []);

  const login = (userData) => {
    localStorage.setItem(
      STORAGE_KEY,
      JSON.stringify(userData)
    );

    setUser(userData);
  };

  const logout = () => {
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    setUser(null);
  };

  const updateUser = (userData) => {
    if (!userData) {
      return;
    }

    localStorage.setItem(
      STORAGE_KEY,
      JSON.stringify(userData)
    );

    setUser(userData);
  };

  const refreshUser = () => {
    const storedUser = readStoredUser();

    setUser(storedUser);

    return storedUser;
  };

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        loading,
        login,
        logout,
        updateUser,
        refreshUser,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAuth() {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error(
      "useAuth deve ser usado dentro de AuthProvider."
    );
  }

  return context;
}

FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\layouts\StudentLayout.jsx
==========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { NavLink, Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  Award,
  BookOpen,
  ChevronRight,
  House,
  LogOut,
  Menu,
  Settings,
  ShieldAlert,
  ShieldCheck,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useAuth } from "../context/AuthContext";

const navigation = [
  {
    label: "Dashboard",
    to: "/student",
    icon: House,
    end: true,
  },
  {
    label: "Cursos",
    to: "/student/cursos",
    icon: BookOpen,
  },
  {
    label: "Simulações",
    to: "/student/simulacoes",
    icon: ShieldAlert,
  },
  {
    label: "Certificados",
    to: "/student/certificados",
    icon: Award,
  },
];

const accountNavigation = [
  {
    label: "Definições",
    to: "/student/definicoes",
    icon: Settings,
  },
];

function getInitials(name) {
  if (!name) return "E";

  const parts = name.trim().split(/\s+/);

  if (parts.length === 1) {
    return parts[0].charAt(0).toUpperCase();
  }

  return `${parts[0].charAt(0)}${parts[parts.length - 1].charAt(0)}`.toUpperCase();
}

export default function StudentLayout() {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();

  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [logoutOpen, setLogoutOpen] = useState(false);

  const fullName = user?.name?.trim() || "Estudante";
  const initials = getInitials(fullName);

  useEffect(() => {
    setMenuOpen(false);
  }, [location.pathname]);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.body.style.overflow = menuOpen || logoutOpen ? "hidden" : "";

    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = "";
    };
  }, [menuOpen, logoutOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleKeyDown(event) {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        setMenuOpen(false);
        setLogoutOpen(false);
      }
    }

    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, []);

  function handleLogout() {
    setLogoutOpen(false);
    setMenuOpen(false);
    logout();
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50">
      {/* ============================================================
          DESKTOP SIDEBAR
      ============================================================ */}
      <aside className="fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-50 hidden w-72 flex-col border-r border-slate-200/80 bg-white xl:flex">
        {/* BRAND */}
        <div className="px-6 pb-6 pt-7">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500 to-blue-700 shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20">
              <ShieldCheck className="h-6 w-6 text-white" />
            </div>

            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="text-xl font-black tracking-tight text-slate-950">
                SICSI <span className="text-blue-600">III</span>
              </p>

              <p className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-slate-400">
                Segurança Digital
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* USER */}
        <div className="px-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 px-3 py-3">
            <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-600 text-xs font-black text-white shadow-md shadow-blue-600/20">
              {initials}
            </div>

            <p
              className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm font-bold text-slate-800"
              title={fullName}
            >
              {fullName}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* NAVIGATION */}
        <nav className="mt-7 flex-1 px-4">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            {navigation.map((item) => (
              <SidebarLink key={item.to} item={item} />
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="my-6 border-t border-slate-100" />

          <div className="space-y-1">
            {accountNavigation.map((item) => (
              <SidebarLink key={item.to} item={item} />
            ))}
          </div>
        </nav>

        {/* LOGOUT */}
        <div className="border-t border-slate-100 p-4">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setLogoutOpen(true)}
            className="group flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500 transition-all hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600"
          >
            <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-50 transition-colors group-hover:bg-red-100">
              <LogOut className="h-4 w-4" />
            </span>

            <span>Sair da conta</span>

            <ChevronRight className="ml-auto h-4 w-4 opacity-0 transition-all group-hover:translate-x-0.5 group-hover:opacity-100" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* ============================================================
          MOBILE/TABLET OVERLAY
      ============================================================ */}
      {menuOpen && (
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label="Fechar menu"
          onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
          className="fixed inset-0 z-40 bg-slate-950/40 backdrop-blur-[2px] xl:hidden"
        />
      )}

      {/* ============================================================
          MOBILE/TABLET SIDEBAR
      ============================================================ */}
      <aside
        className={`fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-50 flex w-[min(86vw,320px)] flex-col border-r border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-2xl transition-transform duration-300 xl:hidden ${
          menuOpen ? "translate-x-0" : "-translate-x-full"
        }`}
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 px-5 py-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-600 shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20">
              <ShieldCheck className="h-5 w-5 text-white" />
            </div>

            <div>
              <p className="font-black tracking-tight text-slate-950">
                SICSI <span className="text-blue-600">III</span>
              </p>

              <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                Segurança Digital
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-50 text-slate-500 transition hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900"
            aria-label="Fechar menu"
          >
            <X className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="px-4 pt-5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-3 py-3">
            <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-600 text-xs font-black text-white">
              {initials}
            </div>

            <p
              className="min-w-0 truncate text-sm font-bold text-slate-800"
              title={fullName}
            >
              {fullName}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <nav className="mt-7 flex-1 px-4">
          <div className="space-y-1">
            {navigation.map((item) => (
              <SidebarLink
                key={item.to}
                item={item}
                onNavigate={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
              />
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="my-6 border-t border-slate-100" />

          <div className="space-y-1">
            {accountNavigation.map((item) => (
              <SidebarLink
                key={item.to}
                item={item}
                onNavigate={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        </nav>

        <div className="border-t border-slate-100 p-4">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              setMenuOpen(false);
              setLogoutOpen(true);
            }}
            className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500 transition hover:bg-red-50 hover:text-red-600"
          >
            <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-50">
              <LogOut className="h-4 w-4" />
            </span>

            Sair da conta
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* ============================================================
          ÁREA PRINCIPAL
      ============================================================ */}
      <div className="min-h-screen xl:ml-72">
        {/* MOBILE HEADER */}
        <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 border-b border-slate-200/80 bg-white/90 px-4 py-3 backdrop-blur-xl xl:hidden">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setMenuOpen(true)}
              className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-blue-50 hover:text-blue-600"
              aria-label="Abrir menu"
            >
              <Menu className="h-5 w-5" />
            </button>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-md shadow-blue-600/20">
                <ShieldCheck className="h-5 w-5" />
              </div>

              <p className="font-black tracking-tight text-slate-950">
                SICSI <span className="text-blue-600">III</span>
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-xs font-black text-blue-700">
              {initials}
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>

        <div className="min-h-[calc(100vh-65px)] xl:min-h-screen">
          <Outlet />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================================
          LOGOUT MODAL
      ============================================================ */}
      {logoutOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/45 px-5 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div
            role="dialog"
            aria-modal="true"
            aria-labelledby="logout-title"
            className="w-full max-w-md overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-2xl"
          >
            <div className="p-6 sm:p-7">
              <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-red-50 text-red-600">
                <LogOut className="h-5 w-5" />
              </div>

              <h2
                id="logout-title"
                className="mt-5 text-xl font-bold text-slate-950"
              >
                Sair da sua conta?
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                A sua sessão será encerrada e voltará para a página de
                autenticação.
              </p>

              <div className="mt-7 flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setLogoutOpen(false)}
                  className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 transition hover:bg-slate-50"
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleLogout}
                  className="rounded-xl bg-red-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow-lg shadow-red-600/20 transition hover:bg-red-700"
                >
                  Sair da conta
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function SidebarLink({ item, onNavigate }) {
  const Icon = item.icon;

  return (
    <NavLink
      to={item.to}
      end={item.end}
      onClick={onNavigate}
      className={({ isActive }) =>
        `group relative flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm font-semibold transition-all duration-200 ${
          isActive
            ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20"
            : "text-slate-500 hover:bg-blue-50 hover:text-blue-700"
        }`
      }
    >
      {({ isActive }) => (
        <>
          <span
            className={`flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl transition-all ${
              isActive
                ? "bg-white/15 text-white"
                : "bg-slate-50 text-slate-500 group-hover:bg-white group-hover:text-blue-600"
            }`}
          >
            <Icon className="h-[18px] w-[18px]" />
          </span>

          <span className="flex-1">{item.label}</span>

          <ChevronRight
            className={`h-4 w-4 transition-all ${
              isActive
                ? "translate-x-0 opacity-100"
                : "-translate-x-1 opacity-0 group-hover:translate-x-0 group-hover:opacity-100"
            }`}
          />

          {isActive && (
            <span className="absolute -right-4 top-1/2 hidden h-8 w-1 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-l-full bg-blue-600 xl:block" />
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </NavLink>
  );
}

import { StrictMode } from "react";
import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
import "./index.css";
import App from "./App";

createRoot(document.getElementById("root")).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <App />
  </StrictMode>
);

FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\pages\admin\AdminDashboardPage.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  BookOpen,
  Users,
  Award,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
  Save,
  Video,
  FileQuestion,
  ShieldCheck,
  UserCog,
} from "lucide-react";

const API = "http://127.0.0.1:8000/api";

async function api(url, options = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(`${API}${url}`, {
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      ...(options.headers || {}),
    },
    ...options,
  });

  const data = await response.json();

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(
      data.detail || "Erro ao comunicar com o servidor."
    );
  }

  return data;
}

export default function AdminDashboardPage() {
  const [stats, setStats] = useState(null);
  const [courses, setCourses] = useState([]);
  const [users, setUsers] = useState([]);
  const [certificates, setCertificates] = useState([]);
  const [selectedCourse, setSelectedCourse] = useState(null);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("courses");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  async function load() {
    const [s, c, u, cert] = await Promise.all([
      api("/admin/stats"),
      api("/admin/courses"),
      api("/users"),
      api("/certificates"),
    ]);

    setStats(s);
    setCourses(c);
    setUsers(u);
    setCertificates(cert);
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    load().catch((error) => setMessage(error.message));
  }, []);

  async function createCourse() {
    const title = window.prompt("Nome do novo curso:");

    if (!title) return;

    const description =
      window.prompt("Descrição do curso:") || "";

    await api("/admin/courses", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({
        title,
        description,
        published: true,
      }),
    });

    await load();
  }

  async function deleteCourse(id) {
    if (!window.confirm("Eliminar este curso?")) return;

    await api(`/admin/courses/${id}`, {
      method: "DELETE",
    });

    if (selectedCourse?.id === id) {
      setSelectedCourse(null);
    }

    await load();
  }

  async function toggleRole(user) {
    const role =
      user.role === "admin"
        ? "student"
        : "admin";

    if (
      !window.confirm(
        `${role === "admin" ? "Promover" : "Remover administrador de"} ${user.name}?`
      )
    ) {
      return;
    }

    await api(`/users/${user.id}/role`, {
      method: "PATCH",
      body: JSON.stringify({ role }),
    });

    await load();
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-white">
      <div className="border-b border-slate-800 bg-slate-900">
        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl items-center justify-between px-5 py-5">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-500">
              SICSI III
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black">
              Administração
            </h1>
          </div>

          <ShieldCheck className="text-blue-500" size={30} />
        </div>
      </div>

      <main className="mx-auto max-w-7xl p-5 sm:p-8">
        <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <Stat icon={Users} label="Utilizadores" value={stats?.users ?? "—"} />
          <Stat icon={BookOpen} label="Cursos" value={stats?.courses ?? "—"} />
          <Stat icon={Award} label="Certificados" value={stats?.certificates ?? "—"} />
          <Stat icon={UserCog} label="Administradores" value={stats?.admins ?? "—"} />
        </div>

        {message && (
          <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl bg-red-500/10 p-4 text-sm text-red-300">
            {message}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="mt-8 flex flex-wrap gap-2 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-2">
          <Tab active={tab === "courses"} onClick={() => setTab("courses")}>
            <BookOpen size={16} />
            Cursos
          </Tab>

          <Tab active={tab === "users"} onClick={() => setTab("users")}>
            <Users size={16} />
            Utilizadores
          </Tab>

          <Tab active={tab === "certificates"} onClick={() => setTab("certificates")}>
            <Award size={16} />
            Certificados
          </Tab>
        </div>

        {tab === "courses" && (
          <section className="mt-6">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-black">
                  Gestão de cursos
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                  Crie cursos, vídeos e testes finais.
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                onClick={createCourse}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold hover:bg-blue-500"
              >
                <Plus size={17} />
                Novo curso
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-6 grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[360px_1fr]">
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {courses.map((course) => (
                  <button
                    key={course.id}
                    onClick={() => setSelectedCourse(course)}
                    className={`w-full rounded-2xl border p-5 text-left ${
                      selectedCourse?.id === course.id
                        ? "border-blue-500 bg-blue-500/10"
                        : "border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex justify-between gap-3">
                      <BookOpen
                        size={20}
                        className="text-blue-500"
                      />

                      <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                        {course.lessons_count} aulas
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <h3 className="mt-4 font-bold">
                      {course.title}
                    </h3>

                    <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
                      {course.description}
                    </p>

                    <p className="mt-4 text-xs text-blue-400">
                      {course.questions_count}/10 perguntas
                    </p>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>

              {selectedCourse ? (
                <CourseEditor
                  course={selectedCourse}
                  onChanged={async () => {
                    const data = await api(
                      `/courses/${selectedCourse.id}`
                    );

                    setSelectedCourse(data);
                    await load();
                  }}
                  onDelete={deleteCourse}
                />
              ) : (
                <div className="flex min-h-[400px] items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-700 bg-slate-900">
                  <div className="text-center">
                    <BookOpen
                      size={42}
                      className="mx-auto text-slate-700"
                    />

                    <p className="mt-4 font-semibold">
                      Seleccione um curso
                    </p>

                    <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
                      O editor aparecerá aqui.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {tab === "users" && (
          <section className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-black">
              Utilizadores
            </h2>

            <div className="mt-6 overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full min-w-[650px] text-left text-sm">
                <thead className="border-b border-slate-800 text-slate-500">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="pb-4">Nome</th>
                    <th className="pb-4">Email</th>
                    <th className="pb-4">Função</th>
                    <th className="pb-4">Acção</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>

                <tbody>
                  {users.map((user) => (
                    <tr
                      key={user.id}
                      className="border-b border-slate-800"
                    >
                      <td className="py-4 font-semibold">
                        {user.name}
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-4 text-slate-500">
                        {user.email}
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-4">
                        <span className="rounded-full bg-blue-500/10 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-blue-400">
                          {user.role === "admin"
                            ? "Administrador"
                            : "Estudante"}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-4">
                        <button
                          onClick={() => toggleRole(user)}
                          className="rounded-lg border border-slate-700 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-800"
                        >
                          {user.role === "admin"
                            ? "Tornar estudante"
                            : "Promover a Admin"}
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </section>
        )}

        {tab === "certificates" && (
          <section className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-black">
              Certificados emitidos
            </h2>

            <div className="mt-6 space-y-3">
              {certificates.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                  Ainda não existem certificados.
                </p>
              ) : (
                certificates.map((certificate) => (
                  <div
                    key={certificate.id}
                    className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center"
                  >
                    <div>
                      <p className="font-bold">
                        {certificate.student_name}
                      </p>

                      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                        {certificate.course_title}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <span className="font-bold text-emerald-400">
                      {certificate.score}%
                    </span>
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}


function CourseEditor({ course, onChanged, onDelete }) {
  const [lessonTitle, setLessonTitle] = useState("");
  const [videoUrl, setVideoUrl] = useState("");

  const [question, setQuestion] = useState("");
  const [options, setOptions] = useState([
    "",
    "",
    "",
    "",
  ]);
  const [correct, setCorrect] = useState(0);
  const [explanation, setExplanation] = useState("");

  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  async function addLesson() {
    if (!lessonTitle || !videoUrl) return;

    setSaving(true);

    try {
      await api(`/admin/courses/${course.id}/lessons`, {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          title: lessonTitle,
          video_url: videoUrl,
        }),
      });

      setLessonTitle("");
      setVideoUrl("");
      await onChanged();
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function removeLesson(id) {
    if (!window.confirm("Remover esta aula?")) return;

    await api(
      `/admin/courses/${course.id}/lessons/${id}`,
      { method: "DELETE" }
    );

    await onChanged();
  }

  async function addQuestion() {
    if (
      !question.trim() ||
      options.some((option) => !option.trim())
    ) {
      alert("Preencha a pergunta e as quatro opções.");
      return;
    }

    await api(`/admin/courses/${course.id}/questions`, {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({
        question,
        options,
        correct_answer: Number(correct),
        explanation,
      }),
    });

    setQuestion("");
    setOptions(["", "", "", ""]);
    setCorrect(0);
    setExplanation("");

    await onChanged();
  }

  async function removeQuestion(id) {
    if (!window.confirm("Remover esta pergunta?")) return;

    await api(
      `/admin/courses/${course.id}/questions/${id}`,
      { method: "DELETE" }
    );

    await onChanged();
  }

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-blue-400">
            Editor
          </p>

          <h2 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black">
            {course.title}
          </h2>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => onDelete(course.id)}
          className="rounded-lg p-2 text-red-400 hover:bg-red-500/10"
        >
          <Trash2 size={18} />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-8">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Video size={19} className="text-blue-500" />
          <h3 className="font-bold">
            Vídeos / Aulas
          </h3>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_1fr_auto]">
          <input
            value={lessonTitle}
            onChange={(e) => setLessonTitle(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Título da aula"
            className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
          />

          <input
            value={videoUrl}
            onChange={(e) => setVideoUrl(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="URL do vídeo"
            className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
          />

          <button
            onClick={addLesson}
            disabled={saving}
            className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold"
          >
            <Plus size={17} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 space-y-2">
          {course.lessons.map((lesson, index) => (
            <div
              key={lesson.id}
              className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <span className="text-xs text-blue-400">
                  {index + 1}
                </span>

                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold">
                    {lesson.title}
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-1 max-w-[420px] truncate text-xs text-slate-600">
                    {lesson.video_url}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                onClick={() => removeLesson(lesson.id)}
                className="text-red-400"
              >
                <Trash2 size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-10 border-t border-slate-800 pt-8">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <FileQuestion size={19} className="text-blue-500" />
          <h3 className="font-bold">
            Teste final — {course.questions_count}/10
          </h3>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 space-y-3">
          <textarea
            value={question}
            onChange={(e) => setQuestion(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Pergunta"
            rows={3}
            className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
          />

          {options.map((option, index) => (
            <div
              key={index}
              className="flex items-center gap-3"
            >
              <input
                type="radio"
                checked={Number(correct) === index}
                onChange={() => setCorrect(index)}
              />

              <input
                value={option}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  const next = [...options];
                  next[index] = e.target.value;
                  setOptions(next);
                }}
                placeholder={`Opção ${index + 1}`}
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              />
            </div>
          ))}

          <textarea
            value={explanation}
            onChange={(e) => setExplanation(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Explicação da resposta"
            rows={3}
            className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
          />

          <button
            onClick={addQuestion}
            disabled={course.questions_count >= 10}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            <Save size={17} />
            Adicionar pergunta
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-5 space-y-2">
          {course.questions.map((item, index) => (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4"
            >
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs text-blue-400">
                  Questão {index + 1}
                </p>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold">
                  {item.question}
                </p>

                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                  Resposta:{" "}
                  {item.options[item.correct_answer]}
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                onClick={() => removeQuestion(item.id)}
                className="shrink-0 text-red-400"
              >
                <Trash2 size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


function Stat({ icon: Icon, label, value }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-5">
      <Icon size={21} className="text-blue-500" />
      <p className="mt-5 text-3xl font-black">{value}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{label}</p>
    </div>
  );
}


function Tab({ active, children, onClick }) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={onClick}
      className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold ${
        active
          ? "bg-blue-600 text-white"
          : "text-slate-500 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-white"
      }`}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}


==========================================================================================
FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\pages\public\AboutPage.jsx
==========================================================================================
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { ShieldCheck, ArrowLeft } from "lucide-react";

export default function AboutPage() {
  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 px-6 py-10 text-white">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl">
        <Link
          to="/"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={16} />
          Voltar
        </Link>

        <div className="mt-14">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-blue-500">
            <ShieldCheck size={30} />
            <span className="font-semibold">SICSI III</span>
          </div>

          <h1 className="mt-6 text-4xl font-black sm:text-5xl">
            Segurança começa com consciência.
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-6 text-lg leading-8 text-slate-400">
            O SICSI III é uma plataforma de conscientização em cibersegurança
            criada para ajudar estudantes a compreender ameaças digitais e
            desenvolver comportamentos mais seguros.
          </p>

          <div className="mt-10 grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
              <h2 className="font-bold">Aprender</h2>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                Cursos e conteúdos orientados para conhecimentos práticos.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
              <h2 className="font-bold">Praticar</h2>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                Simulações interativas para transformar conhecimento em
                decisões seguras.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

function Brand({ light = false }) {
  return (
    <Link
      to="/"
      className="group flex items-center gap-3"
    >
      <div
        className={[
          "flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl shadow-sm transition duration-300 group-hover:scale-105",
          light
            ? "bg-white text-blue-700"
            : "bg-blue-600 text-white",
        ].join(" ")}
      >
        <ShieldCheck size={23} strokeWidth={2} />
      </div>

      <div>
        <p
          className={[
            "text-[17px] font-black tracking-tight",
            light ? "text-white" : "text-slate-950",
          ].join(" ")}
        >
          SICSI III
        </p>

        <p
          className={[
            "text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wide",
            light ? "text-blue-100" : "text-slate-500",
          ].join(" ")}
        >
          Segurança Digital
        </p>
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

function PrimaryButton({ to, children, white = false }) {
  return (
    <Link
      to={to}
      className={[
        "group inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-black transition duration-300 hover:-translate-y-0.5",
        white
          ? "bg-white text-blue-700 shadow-xl shadow-blue-950/20 hover:bg-blue-50"
          : "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-700/20 hover:bg-blue-700",
      ].join(" ")}
    >
      {children}
      <ArrowRight
        size={17}
        className="transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:translate-x-1"
      />
    </Link>
  );
}

function AnchorButton({ href, children, dark = false }) {
  return (
    <a
      href={href}
      className={[
        "group inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-black transition duration-300 hover:-translate-y-0.5",
        dark
          ? "border border-white/30 bg-white/10 text-white hover:bg-white/15"
          : "border border-slate-300 bg-white text-slate-800 hover:border-blue-300 hover:text-blue-700",
      ].join(" ")}
    >
      {children}
      <ChevronRight
        size={17}
        className="transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:translate-x-1"
      />
    </a>
  );
}

                aprendizagem das decisões que podem surgir numa situação
                digital real.
              </p>
            </div>

            <AnchorButton
              href="#recursos"
              dark
            >
              Conhecer os recursos
            </AnchorButton>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* FAQ */}
      <section className="bg-white">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-5 py-20 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 lg:py-24">
          <div className="text-center">
            <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-600">
              Perguntas frequentes
            </p>

            <h2 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-black text-slate-950 sm:text-4xl">
              Antes de começar
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-10 overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-slate-200">
            {faqs.map((item, index) => {
              const isOpen = openFaq === index;

                  key={item.question}
                  className="border-b border-slate-200 last:border-b-0"
                >
                  <button
                    type="button"

                      setOpenFaq(isOpen ? null : index)
                    }

                      <p className="text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
                        {item.answer}

              );

          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

            <ShieldCheck size={28} />
          </div>

          <p className="mt-6 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-300">
            O próximo passo
          </p>

          <h2 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-black tracking-tight sm:text-5xl">
            O conhecimento de hoje
            <span className="block text-blue-400">
              protege o seu amanhã.
            </span>
          </h2>

          <p className="mx-auto mt-5 max-w-2xl text-base leading-7 text-slate-400 sm:text-lg">
            Crie a sua conta e comece o seu percurso de aprendizagem em
            cibersegurança.
          </p>

          <div className="mt-9 flex flex-col justify-center gap-3 sm:flex-row">
            <PrimaryButton to="/registo">
              Criar conta gratuita
            </PrimaryButton>

            <Link
              to="/login"
              className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-white/20 bg-white/5 px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-white/10"
            >
              Já tenho uma conta
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* FOOTER */}
      <footer className="bg-white">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-5 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
          <div className="grid gap-10 md:grid-cols-[1.4fr_0.8fr_0.8fr]">
            <div>
              <Brand />

              <p className="mt-5 max-w-md text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                Plataforma de conscientização em cibersegurança criada para
                ajudar estudantes a compreender ameaças digitais e desenvolver
                comportamentos mais seguros.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div>

                Explorar
              </p>

                  className="text-slate-600 transition hover:text-blue-700"

                </a>

              </p>

                  to="/login"
                  className="text-slate-600 transition hover:text-blue-700"
                >
                  Entrar
                </Link>

                  to="/registo"
                  className="text-slate-600 transition hover:text-blue-700"

                </Link>

import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  ArrowRight,
  CheckCircle2,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  LockKeyhole,
  ShieldCheck,
} from "lucide-react";
import { authService } from "../../services/api";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext";

export default function LoginPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { login } = useAuth();

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    email: "",
    password: "",
  });

  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function handleSubmit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const data = await authService.login(form);

      login(data.user);

      navigate(data.user.role === "admin" ? "/admin" : "/student");
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  const benefits = [
    {
      title: "Continue o seu percurso de aprendizagem",
      description: "Aceda aos seus conteúdos e retome de onde parou.",
    },
    {
      title: "Acompanhe o seu progresso",
      description: "Veja a sua evolução e conquiste novos objectivos.",
    },
    {
      title: "Pratique em situações reais",
      description: "Tome decisões e desenvolva hábitos digitais mais seguros.",
    },
  ];

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-white">
      {/* =========================================================
          50% PAINEL VISUAL + 50% PAINEL DE LOGIN
      ========================================================== */}
      <div className="grid min-h-screen lg:grid-cols-2">
        {/* =========================================================
            PAINEL INSTITUCIONAL
        ========================================================== */}
        <section className="relative hidden min-h-screen overflow-hidden bg-[#06255f] lg:block">
          <div
            className="absolute inset-0 bg-cover bg-center"
            style={{
              backgroundImage:
                "url('https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1516321318423-f06f85e504b3?auto=format&fit=crop&w=1800&q=85')",
            }}
          />

          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-[#031b49]/95 via-[#062b70]/92 to-[#0b4ea2]/78" />

          <div className="absolute inset-0 opacity-20">
            <div
              className="absolute inset-0"
              style={{
                backgroundImage:
                  "linear-gradient(rgba(147,197,253,0.35) 1px, transparent 1px), linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(147,197,253,0.35) 1px, transparent 1px)",
                backgroundSize: "46px 46px",
              }}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="absolute -left-32 top-1/3 h-96 w-96 rounded-full bg-blue-400/15 blur-3xl" />
          <div className="absolute -right-20 bottom-0 h-96 w-96 rounded-full bg-cyan-300/10 blur-3xl" />

          <div className="relative z-10 flex min-h-screen flex-col px-8 py-8 xl:px-12 xl:py-10">
            {/* LOGO */}
            <Link to="/" className="flex w-fit items-center gap-4">
              <div className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-white text-blue-700 shadow-xl shadow-black/20">
                <ShieldCheck size={34} strokeWidth={2.2} />
              </div>

              <div>
                <div className="text-2xl font-black tracking-tight text-white">
                  SICSI III
                </div>

                <div className="mt-0.5 text-sm font-medium tracking-wide text-blue-100">
                  Segurança Digital
                </div>
              </div>
            </Link>

            <div className="mt-3 h-1 w-20 rounded-full bg-blue-300" />

            {/* CONTEÚDO */}
            <div className="my-auto max-w-2xl py-14">
              <p className="mb-6 text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.28em] text-blue-300">
                Segurança começa com consciência.
              </p>

              <h1 className="max-w-2xl text-5xl font-black leading-[0.98] tracking-[-0.035em] text-white xl:text-[68px]">
                Continue a construir
                <span className="block text-blue-300">
                  a sua segurança digital.
                </span>
              </h1>

              <p className="mt-7 max-w-xl text-base leading-8 text-blue-50/85 xl:text-lg">
                Entre na sua conta para continuar o seu percurso de
                aprendizagem, rever conhecimentos e praticar decisões perante
                situações digitais.
              </p>

              <div className="mt-9 space-y-5">
                {benefits.map((benefit) => (
                  <div
                    key={benefit.title}
                    className="flex items-start gap-4"
                  >
                    <div className="mt-0.5 flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-blue-300/20 bg-white/10 text-blue-200 backdrop-blur-sm">
                      <CheckCircle2 size={21} strokeWidth={2} />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-white xl:text-lg">
                        {benefit.title}
                      </h2>

                      <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-blue-100/75 xl:text-[15px]">
                        {benefit.description}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* RODAPÉ */}
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 border-t border-white/10 pt-6 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-blue-300">
                  Conhecimento · Consciência · Acção
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="max-w-xs border-l border-blue-300/40 pl-4 text-sm leading-6 text-blue-100/75">
                Estudantes mais conscientes constroem um futuro digital mais
                seguro.
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* =========================================================
            PAINEL DE LOGIN
        ========================================================== */}
        <section className="relative flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-white px-5 py-8 text-slate-900 sm:px-8 lg:px-10 xl:px-14">
          <div className="absolute left-5 top-6 sm:left-8 lg:left-10 xl:left-14">
            <Link
              to="/"
              className="group inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-2 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500 transition hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-blue-600"
            >
              <ArrowLeft
                size={17}
                className="transition-transform group-hover:-translate-x-0.5"
              />
              Voltar à página inicial
            </Link>
          </div>

          <div className="w-full max-w-md">
            {/* MARCA PARA MOBILE */}
            <div className="mb-9 lg:hidden">
              <Link to="/" className="flex w-fit items-center gap-3">
                <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20">
                  <ShieldCheck size={26} />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <div className="text-lg font-black tracking-tight">
                    SICSI III
                  </div>

                  <div className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">
                    Segurança Digital
                  </div>
                </div>
              </Link>
            </div>

            {/* CABEÇALHO */}
            <div>
              <div className="mb-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-blue-50 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-blue-700">
                <LockKeyhole size={14} />
                Acesso seguro
              </div>

              <h2 className="text-3xl font-black tracking-tight text-slate-950 sm:text-4xl">
                Bem-vindo de volta
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-3 max-w-sm text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500 sm:text-base">
                Entre na sua conta para continuar a sua jornada de
                aprendizagem no SICSI III.
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* ERRO */}
            {error && (
              <div
                role="alert"
                className="mt-7 rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3.5 text-sm leading-6 text-red-700"
              >
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* FORMULÁRIO */}
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-8 space-y-6">
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="email"
                  className="mb-2.5 block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800"
                >
                  Email
                </label>

                <input
                  id="email"
                  type="email"
                  required
                  autoComplete="email"
                  value={form.email}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    setForm({
                      ...form,
                      email: event.target.value,
                    })
                  }
                  placeholder="seu@email.com"
                  className="h-14 w-full rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-4 text-base text-slate-900 outline-none transition placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-blue-500 focus:bg-white focus:ring-4 focus:ring-blue-500/10"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="password"
                  className="mb-2.5 block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800"
                >
                  Palavra-passe
                </label>

                <div className="relative">
                  <input
                    id="password"
                    type={showPassword ? "text" : "password"}
                    required
                    autoComplete="current-password"
                    value={form.password}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      setForm({
                        ...form,
                        password: event.target.value,
                      })
                    }
                    placeholder="Introduza a sua palavra-passe"
                    className="h-14 w-full rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-4 pr-14 text-base text-slate-900 outline-none transition placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-blue-500 focus:bg-white focus:ring-4 focus:ring-blue-500/10"
                  />

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-label={
                      showPassword
                        ? "Ocultar palavra-passe"
                        : "Mostrar palavra-passe"
                    }
                    onClick={() => setShowPassword((current) => !current)}
                    className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 flex h-10 w-10 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700"
                  >
                    {showPassword ? (
                      <EyeOff size={19} />
                    ) : (
                      <Eye size={19} />
                    )}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="group flex h-14 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-blue-600 px-5 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20 transition hover:bg-blue-700 hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-blue-600/25 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <>
                    <span className="h-5 w-5 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-white/40 border-t-white" />
                    A entrar...
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    Entrar na minha conta
                    <ArrowRight
                      size={18}
                      className="transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1"
                    />
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </form>

            {/* SEGURANÇA */}
            <div className="mt-7 rounded-2xl border border-blue-100 bg-blue-50/70 p-4">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <div className="mt-0.5 flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white text-blue-600 shadow-sm">
                  <ShieldCheck size={18} />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">
                    Acesso protegido
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                    Os seus dados de acesso são tratados através do sistema de
                    autenticação do SICSI III.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* REGISTO */}
            <p className="mt-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
              Ainda não tem conta?{" "}
              <Link
                to="/registo"
                className="font-bold text-blue-600 transition hover:text-blue-700"
              >
                Criar conta
              </Link>
            </p>

            <p className="mt-6 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">
              SICSI III · Segurança Digital
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
