FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\pages\public\RegisterPage.jsx
==========================================================================================
import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  ArrowRight,
  CheckCircle2,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  LockKeyhole,
  Mail,
  ShieldCheck,
  UserRound,
} from "lucide-react";
import { authService } from "../../services/api";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext";

const benefits = [
  "Descubra o seu nível de consciência digital",
  "Receba um percurso orientado de aprendizagem",
  "Pratique através de experiências interactivas",
  "Acompanhe a sua evolução no SICSI III",
];

function PasswordField({
  id,
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  show,
  onToggle,
  autoComplete,
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label
        htmlFor={id}
        className="mb-2 block text-sm font-bold text-slate-800"
      >
        {label}
      </label>

      <div className="relative">
        <LockKeyhole
          size={18}
          className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400"
        />

        <input
          id={id}
          type={show ? "text" : "password"}
          required
          autoComplete={autoComplete}
          value={value}
          onChange={onChange}
          placeholder="••••••••"
          className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white py-3.5 pl-11 pr-12 text-sm text-slate-950 outline-none transition placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-blue-500/10"
        />

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onToggle}
          className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-lg p-2 text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700"
          aria-label={
            show
              ? "Ocultar palavra-passe"
              : "Mostrar palavra-passe"
          }
        >
          {show ? (
            <EyeOff size={18} />
          ) : (
            <Eye size={18} />
          )}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function RegisterPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { login } = useAuth();

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    name: "",
    email: "",
    password: "",
    confirmPassword: "",
  });

  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [showConfirmPassword, setShowConfirmPassword] =
    useState(false);

  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function handleSubmit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setError("");

    if (form.password !== form.confirmPassword) {
      setError("As palavras-passe não coincidem.");
      return;
    }

    if (form.password.length < 6) {
      setError(
        "A palavra-passe deve ter pelo menos 6 caracteres."
      );
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const data = await authService.register({
        name: form.name,
        email: form.email,
        password: form.password,
      });

      login(data.user);

      navigate("/student/autoavaliacao");
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err?.message ||
          "Não foi possível criar a sua conta."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-white text-slate-950">
      <div className="grid min-h-screen lg:grid-cols-[0.98fr_1.02fr]">
        {/* FORM */}
        <section className="relative order-2 flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center px-5 py-10 sm:px-8 lg:order-1 lg:px-12">
          <div className="absolute left-0 top-0 h-64 w-64 rounded-full bg-blue-50 blur-3xl" />

          <div className="relative w-full max-w-md">
            <div className="mb-8">
              <Link
                to="/"
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-3"
              >
                <div className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm">
                  <ShieldCheck size={23} />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <p className="font-black text-slate-950">
                    SICSI III
                  </p>

                  <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500">
                    Segurança Digital
                  </p>
                </div>
              </Link>
            </div>

            <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-blue-600">
              Comece aqui
            </p>

            <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-black tracking-tight text-slate-950 sm:text-4xl">
              Crie a sua conta.
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500 sm:text-base">
              Dê o primeiro passo para desenvolver uma consciência digital
              mais forte.
            </p>

            {error && (
              <div
                role="alert"
                className="mt-7 rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-4 text-sm leading-6 text-red-700"
              >
                <p className="font-bold">
                  Verifique os dados
                </p>

                <p className="mt-1">
                  {error}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}

            <form
              onSubmit={handleSubmit}
              className="mt-8 space-y-5"
            >
              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="register-name"
                  className="mb-2 block text-sm font-bold text-slate-800"
                >
                  Nome completo
                </label>

                <div className="relative">
                  <UserRound
                    size={18}
                    className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400"
                  />

                  <input
                    id="register-name"
                    type="text"
                    required
                    autoComplete="name"
                    value={form.name}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      setForm({
                        ...form,
                        name: event.target.value,
                      })
                    }
                    placeholder="O seu nome completo"
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white py-3.5 pl-11 pr-4 text-sm text-slate-950 outline-none transition placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-blue-500/10"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label
                  htmlFor="register-email"
                  className="mb-2 block text-sm font-bold text-slate-800"
                >
                  Email
                </label>

                <div className="relative">
                  <Mail
                    size={18}
                    className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400"
                  />

                  <input
                    id="register-email"
                    type="email"
                    required
                    autoComplete="email"
                    value={form.email}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      setForm({
                        ...form,
                        email: event.target.value,
                      })
                    }
                    placeholder="seu@email.com"
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white py-3.5 pl-11 pr-4 text-sm text-slate-950 outline-none transition placeholder:text-slate-400 focus:border-blue-500 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-blue-500/10"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <PasswordField
                id="register-password"
                label="Palavra-passe"
                value={form.password}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setForm({
                    ...form,
                    password: event.target.value,
                  })
                }
                show={showPassword}
                onToggle={() =>
                  setShowPassword((value) => !value)
                }
                autoComplete="new-password"
              />

              <PasswordField
                id="register-confirm-password"
                label="Confirmar palavra-passe"
                value={form.confirmPassword}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setForm({
                    ...form,
                    confirmPassword: event.target.value,
                  })
                }
                show={showConfirmPassword}
                onToggle={() =>
                  setShowConfirmPassword(
                    (value) => !value
                  )
                }
                autoComplete="new-password"
              />

              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4">
                <div className="flex gap-3">
                  <LockKeyhole
                    size={18}
                    className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-blue-600"
                  />

                  <div>
                    <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
                      Palavra-passe segura
                    </p>

                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                      Utilize pelo menos 6 caracteres e evite partilhar as
                      suas credenciais.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="group flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 py-3.5 text-sm font-black text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20 transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60 disabled:hover:translate-y-0"
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <>
                    <span className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-white/30 border-t-white" />
                    A criar conta...
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    Criar a minha conta
                    <ArrowRight
                      size={17}
                      className="transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1"
                    />
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </form>

            <p className="mt-7 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
              Já tem uma conta?{" "}
              <Link
                to="/login"
                className="font-black text-blue-600 transition hover:text-blue-700"
              >
                Entrar
              </Link>
            </p>

            <Link
              to="/"
              className="mx-auto mt-5 block w-fit text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 transition hover:text-blue-600"
            >
              Voltar à página inicial
            </Link>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* VISUAL */}
        <section className="relative order-1 hidden overflow-hidden bg-blue-950 lg:order-2 lg:block">
          <img
            src="https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1523240795612-9a054b0db644?auto=format&fit=crop&w=1800&q=90"
            alt="Estudantes em aprendizagem"
            className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover"
          />

          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-950/90 via-blue-950/55 to-blue-900/20" />

          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[linear-gradient(rgba(59,130,246,0.10)_1px,transparent_1px),linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(59,130,246,0.10)_1px,transparent_1px)] bg-[size:64px_64px]" />

          <div className="relative flex min-h-screen flex-col justify-between p-10 xl:p-14">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white text-blue-700 shadow-lg">
                <ShieldCheck size={25} />
              </div>

              <div>
                <p className="text-lg font-black text-white">
                  SICSI III
                </p>

                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-blue-200">
                  Segurança Digital
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="max-w-xl">
              <div className="mb-6 h-1 w-20 rounded-full bg-blue-400" />

              <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-blue-300">
                O seu percurso começa aqui.
              </p>

              <h2 className="mt-5 text-4xl font-black leading-tight tracking-tight text-white xl:text-6xl">
                Aprenda. Pratique. Esteja preparado.
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-6 max-w-lg text-base leading-7 text-blue-100/80 xl:text-lg">
                Ao criar a sua conta, começa por descobrir o seu nível de
                consciência digital e passa a ter acesso ao seu percurso de
                aprendizagem.
              </p>

              <div className="mt-8 grid gap-3">
                {benefits.map((benefit) => (
                  <div
                    key={benefit}
                    className="flex items-start gap-3 text-sm text-blue-50"
                  >
                    <CheckCircle2
                      size={17}
                      className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-blue-300"
                    />

                    <span>{benefit}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <p className="text-xs font-medium text-blue-200/60">
              Conhecimento · Consciência · Acção
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

==========================================================================================
FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\pages\student\AssessmentPage.jsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  ArrowRight,
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  CircleCheck,
  Loader2,
  ShieldCheck,
  Sparkles,
  Target,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext";

function getErrorMessage(error, fallback = "Ocorreu um erro. Tente novamente.") {
  const detail = error?.response?.data?.detail ?? error?.message;

  if (typeof detail === "string") return detail;

  if (Array.isArray(detail)) {
    return detail
      .map((item) => item?.msg || item?.message || String(item))
      .join(" ");
  }

  if (detail && typeof detail === "object") {
    return detail.msg || detail.message || fallback;
  }

  return fallback;
}

function normalizeRecommendations(value) {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];

  return value
    .map((item) => {
      if (typeof item === "string") return item;
      if (item && typeof item === "object") {
        return item.title || item.name || item.course || "";
      }
      return "";
    })
    .filter(Boolean);
}

export default function AssessmentPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user, updateUser } = useAuth();

  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState([]);
  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState([]);
  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(0);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [result, setResult] = useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    async function loadQuestions() {
      try {
        setLoading(true);
        setError("");

        const response = await fetch("http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/assessment/questions");

        if (!response.ok) {
          throw new Error("Não foi possível carregar a autoavaliação.");
        }

        const data = await response.json();

        if (!active) return;

        const loadedQuestions = Array.isArray(data)
          ? data
          : Array.isArray(data?.questions)
            ? data.questions
            : [];

        setQuestions(loadedQuestions);
        setAnswers(new Array(loadedQuestions.length).fill(""));
      } catch (err) {
        if (active) {
          setError(
            getErrorMessage(
              err,
              "Não foi possível carregar a autoavaliação. Verifique a ligação ao servidor."
            )
          );
        }
      } finally {
        if (active) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    loadQuestions();

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const currentQuestion = questions[currentIndex];

  const answeredCount = useMemo(
    () => answers.filter((answer) => answer !== "").length,
    [answers]
  );

  const progress =
    questions.length > 0
      ? Math.round((answeredCount / questions.length) * 100)
      : 0;

  const questionProgress =
    questions.length > 0
      ? Math.round(((currentIndex + 1) / questions.length) * 100)
      : 0;

  function handleAnswer(value) {
    setAnswers((previous) => {
      const next = [...previous];
      next[currentIndex] = value;
      return next;
    });

    setError("");
  }

  function handlePrevious() {
    if (currentIndex > 0) {
      setCurrentIndex((previous) => previous - 1);
      setError("");
    }
  }

  function handleNext() {
    if (!currentQuestion) return;

    if (answers[currentIndex] === "") {
      setError("Seleccione uma resposta antes de continuar.");
      return;
    }

    if (currentIndex < questions.length - 1) {
      setCurrentIndex((previous) => previous + 1);
      setError("");
    }
  }

  async function handleSubmit() {
    if (answers.some((answer) => answer === "")) {
      setError("Responda a todas as perguntas antes de concluir.");
      return;
    }

    if (!user?.id) {
      setError("Não foi possível identificar o utilizador autenticado.");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setSubmitting(true);
      setError("");

      const normalizedAnswers = answers.map((answer) => String(answer));

      const response = await fetch(
        "http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/assessment/submit",
        {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            user_id: user.id,
            answers: normalizedAnswers,
          }),
        }
      );

      const data = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          typeof data?.detail === "string"
            ? data.detail
            : "Não foi possível concluir a autoavaliação."
        );
      }

      const recommendations = normalizeRecommendations(
        data.recommendations
      );

      const updatedUser = {
        ...user,
        initial_assessment_completed: true,
        assessment_score: data.percentage ?? data.score ?? 0,
        awareness_level: data.level || "",
        recommendations,
      };

      updateUser(updatedUser);
      setResult({
        ...data,
        recommendations,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        getErrorMessage(
          err,
          "Não foi possível concluir a autoavaliação. Tente novamente."
        )
      );
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 flex items-center justify-center px-6">
        <div className="text-center">
          <div className="mx-auto mb-5 flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-blue-600 shadow-xl shadow-blue-600/20">
            <ShieldCheck className="h-8 w-8 text-white" />
          </div>

          <Loader2 className="mx-auto h-6 w-6 animate-spin text-blue-600" />

          <p className="mt-4 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">
            A preparar a sua autoavaliação...
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (result) {
    const score = Number(result.percentage ?? result.score ?? 0);

    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen overflow-hidden bg-white">
        <div className="relative">
          <div className="absolute inset-x-0 top-0 h-80 bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-50 via-white to-sky-50" />

          <div className="relative mx-auto max-w-6xl px-5 py-10 sm:px-8 lg:px-10 lg:py-16">
            <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl">
              <div className="mb-8 flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-600 shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20">
                  <ShieldCheck className="h-6 w-6 text-white" />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-blue-600">
                    SICSI
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                    Perfil de consciência digital
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-2xl shadow-slate-200/70">
                <div className="border-b border-slate-100 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-blue-700 px-6 py-10 text-white sm:px-10">
                  <div className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-white/15 ring-1 ring-white/20">
                    <CheckCircle2 className="h-9 w-9" />
                  </div>

                  <p className="mt-7 text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-blue-100">
                    Autoavaliação concluída
                  </p>

                  <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">
                    O seu perfil de consciência digital está pronto.
                  </h1>

                  <p className="mt-4 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-blue-100 sm:text-base">
                    Este resultado ajuda o SICSI a orientar a sua aprendizagem
                    e apresentar conteúdos mais adequados ao seu nível actual.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="grid gap-6 p-6 sm:p-10 md:grid-cols-[220px_1fr]">
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-7 text-center ring-1 ring-slate-100">
                    <div className="relative flex h-36 w-36 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white shadow-inner ring-8 ring-blue-50">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-4xl font-bold text-blue-600">
                          {score}%
                        </p>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                          resultado
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-blue-100 bg-blue-50/60 p-6">
                      <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-blue-600">
                        Nível identificado
                      </p>

                      <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
                        {result.level || "Perfil identificado"}
                      </h2>

                      <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
                        A sua classificação será utilizada para orientar a
                        experiência de aprendizagem dentro do SICSI.
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    {result.recommendations?.length > 0 && (
                      <div className="mt-6">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <Sparkles className="h-5 w-5 text-blue-600" />
                          <h3 className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                            Recomendações para si
                          </h3>
                        </div>

                        <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                          {result.recommendations.map((recommendation, index) => (
                            <div
                              key={`${recommendation}-${index}`}
                              className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-4 transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-blue-200 hover:shadow-md"
                            >
                              <div className="mt-0.5 flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                                <Check className="h-4 w-4" />
                              </div>

                              <p className="text-sm font-medium leading-5 text-slate-700">
                                {recommendation}
                              </p>
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => navigate("/student")}
                      className="mt-8 inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20 transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:bg-blue-700 hover:shadow-xl sm:w-auto"
                    >
                      Ir para o Dashboard
                      <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error && questions.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 px-5 py-10 sm:px-8">
        <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[80vh] max-w-xl items-center justify-center">
          <div className="w-full rounded-3xl border border-red-100 bg-white p-8 text-center shadow-xl shadow-slate-200/60">
            <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-red-50">
              <ShieldCheck className="h-7 w-7 text-red-500" />
            </div>

            <h1 className="mt-5 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
              Não foi possível carregar
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">{error}</p>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => window.location.reload()}
              className="mt-7 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20 transition hover:bg-blue-700"
            >
              Tentar novamente
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50">
      <div className="relative overflow-hidden bg-white">
        <div className="absolute -right-32 -top-32 h-72 w-72 rounded-full bg-blue-100/60 blur-3xl" />
        <div className="absolute -left-32 top-24 h-72 w-72 rounded-full bg-sky-100/50 blur-3xl" />

        <div className="relative mx-auto max-w-6xl px-5 pb-10 pt-7 sm:px-8 lg:px-10 lg:pb-14 lg:pt-10">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-600 shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20">
                <ShieldCheck className="h-6 w-6 text-white" />
              </div>

              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
                  SICSI
                </p>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                  Segurança e consciência digital
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="hidden items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-slate-200 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 shadow-sm sm:flex">
              <Target className="h-4 w-4 text-blue-600" />
              Avaliação inicial
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-12 grid gap-10 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_320px] lg:items-end">
            <div className="max-w-3xl">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-blue-100 bg-blue-50 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-blue-700">
                <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4" />
                Descubra o seu nível de consciência digital
              </div>

              <h1 className="mt-5 text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-950 sm:text-5xl lg:text-6xl">
                Conhecer o seu nível é o primeiro passo para{" "}
                <span className="text-blue-600">proteger-se melhor.</span>
              </h1>

              <p className="mt-5 max-w-2xl text-base leading-7 text-slate-600 sm:text-lg">
                Responda às perguntas com base no que realmente faria em cada
                situação. Não é um exame de aprovação ou reprovação. O
                objectivo é compreender o seu nível actual e personalizar a
                sua aprendizagem.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-xl shadow-slate-200/50">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-slate-400">
                    Progresso
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
                    {progress}%
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                  <CircleCheck className="h-6 w-6" />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-5 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-100">
                <div
                  className="h-full rounded-full bg-blue-600 transition-all duration-500"
                  style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}
                />
              </div>

              <p className="mt-3 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">
                {answeredCount} de {questions.length} perguntas respondidas
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <main className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-5 py-8 sm:px-8 lg:px-10 lg:py-12">
        <div className="grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_280px]">
          <section>
            <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-blue-600">
                  Pergunta {currentIndex + 1}
                </p>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-medium text-slate-500">
                  de {questions.length}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="text-right">
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400">
                  Progresso da avaliação
                </p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-slate-700">
                  {questionProgress}%
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-xl shadow-slate-200/60">
              <div className="h-1.5 bg-slate-100">
                <div
                  className="h-full bg-blue-600 transition-all duration-500"
                  style={{ width: `${questionProgress}%` }}
                />
              </div>

              <div className="p-6 sm:p-9 lg:p-10">
                <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                  <ShieldCheck className="h-6 w-6" />
                </div>

                <h2 className="mt-7 text-2xl font-bold leading-tight tracking-tight text-slate-950 sm:text-3xl">
                  {currentQuestion?.question ||
                    currentQuestion?.text ||
                    currentQuestion?.title ||
                    ""}
                </h2>

                {currentQuestion?.description && (
                  <p className="mt-4 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                    {currentQuestion.description}
                  </p>
                )}

                <div className="mt-8 space-y-3">
                  {(currentQuestion?.options || []).map((option, optionIndex) => {
                    const value =
                      typeof option === "object"
                        ? String(option.id ?? option.value ?? optionIndex)
                        : String(optionIndex);

                    const label =
                      typeof option === "object"
                        ? option.text ?? option.label ?? option.title ?? ""
                        : option;

                    const selected = answers[currentIndex] === value;

                    return (
                      <button
                        key={value}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleAnswer(value)}
                        className={`group flex w-full items-start gap-4 rounded-2xl border p-4 text-left transition-all duration-200 sm:p-5 ${
                          selected
                            ? "border-blue-500 bg-blue-50 shadow-md shadow-blue-100"
                            : "border-slate-200 bg-white hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-blue-200 hover:bg-blue-50/30 hover:shadow-md"
                        }`}
                      >
                        <span
                          className={`flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border text-xs font-bold transition ${
                            selected
                              ? "border-blue-600 bg-blue-600 text-white"
                              : "border-slate-300 bg-white text-slate-500 group-hover:border-blue-400 group-hover:text-blue-600"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {String.fromCharCode(65 + optionIndex)}
                        </span>

                        <span
                          className={`flex-1 pt-0.5 text-sm font-medium leading-6 sm:text-base ${
                            selected ? "text-blue-950" : "text-slate-700"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {label}
                        </span>

                        <span
                          className={`mt-0.5 flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full ${
                            selected
                              ? "bg-blue-600 text-white"
                              : "bg-slate-100 text-transparent group-hover:bg-blue-100"
                          }`}
                        >
                          <Check className="h-4 w-4" />
                        </span>
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>

                {error && (
                  <div className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium leading-5 text-red-700">
                    {error}
                  </div>
                )}

                <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 border-t border-slate-100 pt-6 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handlePrevious}
                    disabled={currentIndex === 0 || submitting}
                    className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 transition hover:border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
                  >
                    <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
                    Anterior
                  </button>

                  {currentIndex < questions.length - 1 ? (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={handleNext}
                      disabled={submitting}
                      className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20 transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:bg-blue-700 hover:shadow-xl disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                    >
                      Próxima pergunta
                      <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </button>
                  ) : (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={handleSubmit}
                      disabled={submitting}
                      className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20 transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:bg-blue-700 hover:shadow-xl disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
                    >
                      {submitting ? (
                        <>
                          <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
                          A calcular resultado...
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          Concluir autoavaliação
                          <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                        </>
                      )}
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <aside className="hidden lg:block">
            <div className="sticky top-8 space-y-4">
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-lg shadow-slate-200/50">
                <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-slate-400">
                  Percurso
                </p>

                <div className="mt-5 space-y-3">
                  {questions.map((_, index) => {
                    const answered = answers[index] !== "";
                    const active = index === currentIndex;

                    return (
                      <button
                        key={index}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setCurrentIndex(index)}
                        className={`flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-left transition ${
                          active
                            ? "bg-blue-50 text-blue-700"
                            : "hover:bg-slate-50"
                        }`}
                      >
                        <span
                          className={`flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-xs font-bold ${
                            answered
                              ? "bg-blue-600 text-white"
                              : active
                                ? "border border-blue-200 bg-white text-blue-600"
                                : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-400"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {answered ? (
                            <Check className="h-4 w-4" />
                          ) : (
                            index + 1
                          )}
                        </span>

                        <span className="text-xs font-semibold">
                          Pergunta {index + 1}
                        </span>
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-blue-100 bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-50 to-white p-5">
                <ShieldCheck className="h-6 w-6 text-blue-600" />

                <h3 className="mt-4 font-bold text-slate-900">
                  Responda com honestidade
                </h3>

                <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-600">
                  Não procure acertar. As respostas servem para compreender o
                  seu nível e personalizar a experiência no SICSI.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

==========================================================================================
FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\pages\student\CertificatesPage.jsx

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  Award,
  Download,
  Eye,
  ShieldCheck,
  Printer,
} from "lucide-react";
import { certificateService } from "../../services/api";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext";

export default function CertificatesPage() {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [certificates, setCertificates] =
    useState([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  const [selected, setSelected] =
    useState(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    certificateService
      .getUserCertificates(user.id)
      .then(setCertificates)
      .finally(() =>
        setLoading(false)
      );
  }, [user.id]);

  async function downloadCertificate(
    certificate
  ) {
    try {
      const blob =
        await certificateService.download(
          certificate.id
        );

      const url =
        window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);

      const link =
        document.createElement("a");

      link.href = url;
      link.download =
        `${certificate.certificate_code}.pdf`;

      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      link.remove();

      window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    } catch (error) {
      alert(error.message);
    }
  }

  function printCertificate(
    certificate
  ) {
    const url =
      `http://localhost:8000/api/certificates/${certificate.id}/pdf`;

    const windowRef =
      window.open(url, "_blank");

    if (!windowRef) {
      alert(
        "Permita pop-ups para visualizar o certificado."
      );
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-5 sm:p-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl">
        <p className="text-sm text-blue-400">
          Conquistas
        </p>

        <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black">
          Certificados
        </h1>

        <p className="mt-2 text-slate-500">
          Consulte, visualize e baixe os certificados
          conquistados.
        </p>

        {loading ? (
          <p className="mt-8 text-slate-500">
            A carregar certificados...
          </p>
        ) : certificates.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-700 p-12 text-center">
            <Award
              className="mx-auto text-slate-600"
              size={45}
            />

            <p className="mt-5 font-semibold">
              Ainda não possui certificados.
            </p>

            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
              Conclua um curso e seja aprovado no
              teste final para receber o seu primeiro
              certificado.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="mt-8 grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
            {certificates.map(
              (certificate) => (
                <div
                  key={certificate.id}
                  className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-blue-500/20 bg-slate-900"
                >
                  <div className="relative p-7">
                    <div className="absolute right-5 top-5 text-blue-500/10">
                      <ShieldCheck size={90} />
                    </div>

                    <Award
                      className="text-blue-500"
                      size={34}
                    />

                    <p className="mt-5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-400">
                      Certificado SICSI III
                    </p>

                    <h2 className="mt-3 max-w-md text-2xl font-black">
                      {certificate.course_title}
                    </h2>

                    <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                      <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-950 p-4">
                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                          Aproveitamento
                        </p>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xl font-bold">
                          {certificate.score}%
                        </p>
                      </div>

                      <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-950 p-4">
                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                          Código
                        </p>
                        <p className="mt-1 truncate font-mono text-xs font-bold text-blue-400">
                          {certificate.certificate_code}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-3 border-t border-slate-800">
                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        setSelected(
                          certificate
                        )
                      }
                      className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 p-4 text-sm text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-800"
                    >
                      <Eye size={17} />
                      Ver
                    </button>

                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        printCertificate(
                          certificate
                        )
                      }
                      className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border-x border-slate-800 p-4 text-sm text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-800"
                    >
                      <Printer size={17} />
                      Abrir PDF
                    </button>

                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        downloadCertificate(
                          certificate
                        )
                      }
                      className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 p-4 text-sm font-semibold text-blue-400 hover:bg-blue-500/10"
                    >
                      <Download size={17} />
                      Baixar
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {selected && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-950/80 p-5 backdrop-blur-sm">
          <div className="w-full max-w-2xl rounded-3xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 p-8 shadow-2xl">
            <div className="text-center">
              <Award
                className="mx-auto text-blue-500"
                size={45}
              />

              <p className="mt-5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-400">
                Certificado de conclusão
              </p>

              <h2 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-black">
                {selected.course_title}
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-6 text-sm text-slate-500">
                Certificamos que
              </p>

              <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold">
                {selected.student_name}
              </p>

              <p className="mx-auto mt-5 max-w-lg text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
                concluiu com aproveitamento o curso
                acima indicado no âmbito da plataforma
                SICSI III.
              </p>

              <div className="mt-7 grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-950 p-4">
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                    Nota
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xl font-bold">
                    {selected.score}%
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-950 p-4">
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                    Código
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-xs font-bold text-blue-400">
                    {selected.certificate_code}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-7 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
              <button
                onClick={() =>
                  printCertificate(
                    selected
                  )
                }
                className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-slate-700"
              >
                <Printer size={17} />
                Abrir PDF
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={() =>
                  downloadCertificate(
                    selected
                  )
                }
                className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-blue-500"
              >
                <Download size={17} />
                Baixar certificado
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={() =>
                  setSelected(null)
                }
                className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 px-5 py-3 text-sm text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              >
                Fechar
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


==========================================================================================
FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\pages\student\CourseLearningPage.jsx
==========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  AlertCircle,
  ArrowLeft,
  ArrowRight,
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  CirclePlay,
  ListVideo,
  PlayCircle,
  ShieldCheck,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext";
import { courseService } from "../../services/api";

function getVideoEmbedUrl(url) {
  if (!url) {
    return "";
  }

  const value = String(url).trim();

  if (value.includes("youtube.com/watch")) {
    try {
      const parsed = new URL(value);
      const videoId = parsed.searchParams.get("v");

      if (videoId) {
        return `https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/${videoId}?rel=0`;
      }
    } catch {
      return "";
    }
  }

  if (value.includes("youtu.be/")) {
    try {
      const parsed = new URL(value);
      const videoId = parsed.pathname.replace("/", "").split("/")[0];

      if (videoId) {
        return `https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/${videoId}?rel=0`;
      }
    } catch {
      return "";
    }
  }

  if (value.includes("youtube.com/embed/")) {
    return value;
  }

  return "";
}

export default function CourseLearningPage() {
  const { courseId } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user, updateUser } = useAuth();

  const [course, setCourse] = useState(null);
  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(0);
  const [completedLessons, setCompletedLessons] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    async function loadCourse() {
      try {
        setLoading(true);
        setError("");

        const data = await courseService.getCourse(courseId);

        if (!active) {
          return;
        }

        setCourse(data);

        const storedCompleted =
          user?.completed_lessons?.[courseId] || [];

        setCompletedLessons(
          Array.isArray(storedCompleted)
            ? storedCompleted
            : []
        );
      } catch (err) {
        if (active) {
          setError(
            err.message || "Não foi possível carregar o curso."
          );
        }
      } finally {
        if (active) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    loadCourse();

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [courseId]);

  const lessons = useMemo(() => {
    if (!course?.lessons) {
      return [];
    }

    return [...course.lessons].sort(
      (a, b) =>
        Number(a.order || 0) - Number(b.order || 0)
    );
  }, [course]);

  const currentLesson = lessons[currentIndex] || null;

  const completedCount = completedLessons.filter((id) =>
    lessons.some((lesson) => lesson.id === id)
  ).length;

  const progress = lessons.length
    ? Math.round((completedCount / lessons.length) * 100)
    : 0;

  const isCurrentCompleted =
    currentLesson &&
    completedLessons.includes(currentLesson.id);

  async function completeCurrentLesson() {
    if (!currentLesson || !user?.id || saving) {
      return false;
    }

    if (completedLessons.includes(currentLesson.id)) {
      return true;
    }

    try {
      setSaving(true);
      setError("");

      const result = await courseService.completeLesson(
        courseId,
        user.id,
        currentLesson.id
      );

      const updatedCompleted =
        Array.isArray(result.completed_lessons)
          ? result.completed_lessons
          : [...completedLessons, currentLesson.id];

      setCompletedLessons(updatedCompleted);

      updateUser({
        ...user,
        completed_lessons: {
          ...(user.completed_lessons || {}),
          [courseId]: updatedCompleted,
        },
        ...(result.course_completed
          ? {
              completed_courses: [
                ...(user.completed_courses || []).filter(
                  (id) => id !== courseId
                ),
                courseId,
              ],
            }
          : {}),
      });

      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.message ||
          "Não foi possível guardar o progresso desta aula."
      );

      return false;
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function nextLesson() {
    if (!currentLesson) {
      return;
    }

    const completed = await completeCurrentLesson();

    if (!completed) {
      return;
    }

    if (currentIndex < lessons.length - 1) {
      setCurrentIndex((index) => index + 1);

      window.scrollTo({
        top: 0,
        behavior: "smooth",
      });

      return;
    }

    navigate(`/student/cursos/${courseId}/teste`);
  }

  function previousLesson() {
    if (currentIndex === 0) {
      return;
    }

    setCurrentIndex((index) => index - 1);

    window.scrollTo({
      top: 0,
      behavior: "smooth",
    });
  }

  function goToLesson(index) {
    if (index < 0 || index >= lessons.length) {
      return;
    }

    setCurrentIndex(index);
    setError("");

    window.scrollTo({
      top: 0,
      behavior: "smooth",
    });
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-[70vh] bg-slate-50 px-4 py-6 sm:px-6">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl animate-pulse">
          <div className="h-5 w-36 rounded bg-slate-200" />
          <div className="mt-5 aspect-video rounded-3xl bg-slate-200" />
          <div className="mt-4 h-40 rounded-3xl bg-white" />
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error && !course) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-[70vh] bg-slate-50 px-4 py-10 sm:px-6">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-white p-8 text-center shadow-sm">
          <AlertCircle className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-red-500" />

          <h1 className="mt-4 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
            Não foi possível carregar o curso
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
            {error}
          </p>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => navigate("/student/cursos")}
            className="mt-6 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white"
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={16} />
            Voltar aos cursos
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!course || !currentLesson) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-[70vh] bg-slate-50 px-4 py-10 sm:px-6">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-8 text-center shadow-sm">
          <ShieldCheck className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-blue-600" />

          <h1 className="mt-4 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
            Curso sem aulas disponíveis
          </h1>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const embedUrl = getVideoEmbedUrl(
    currentLesson.video_url
  );

  const previousIndex = currentIndex - 1;
  const nextIndex = currentIndex + 1;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 px-4 py-5 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl">

        {/* BACK */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => navigate("/student/cursos")}
          className="mb-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500 transition hover:text-blue-600"
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={16} />
          Voltar aos cursos
        </button>

        {/* VIDEO */}
        <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-[1.5rem] border border-slate-200 bg-slate-950 shadow-xl">
          <div className="aspect-video w-full max-h-[68vh]">
            {embedUrl ? (
              <iframe
                key={embedUrl}
                src={embedUrl}
                title={currentLesson.title}
                className="h-full w-full"
                loading="lazy"
                allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share"
                allowFullScreen
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center p-8 text-center text-white">
                <div>
                  <PlayCircle className="mx-auto h-14 w-14 text-slate-500" />

                  <p className="mt-4 font-bold">
                    Vídeo indisponível
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-400">
                    O endereço do vídeo desta aula não é válido.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* LESSON HEADER BELOW VIDEO */}
        <section className="mt-4 rounded-[1.5rem] border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
          <div className="p-5 sm:p-6">

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 lg:flex-row lg:items-start lg:justify-between">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="rounded-full bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-black text-blue-700">
                    Aula {currentIndex + 1} de {lessons.length}
                  </span>

                  {isCurrentCompleted && (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-emerald-700">
                      <Check size={13} />
                      Concluída
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                <h1 className="mt-3 text-2xl font-black tracking-tight text-slate-950 sm:text-3xl">
                  {currentLesson.title}
                </h1>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                  {course.title}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="w-full shrink-0 lg:w-64">
                <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-500">
                    Progresso
                  </span>

                  <span className="font-black text-blue-600">
                    {progress}%
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-100">
                  <div
                    className="h-full rounded-full bg-blue-600 transition-all duration-500"
                    style={{
                      width: `${progress}%`,
                    }}
                  />
                </div>

                <p className="mt-2 text-right text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-400">
                  {completedCount} de {lessons.length} aulas concluídas
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* DESKTOP LESSON NAVIGATION */}
          <div className="hidden border-t border-slate-100 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6 md:block">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 text-xs font-black text-slate-700">
                <ListVideo size={17} className="text-blue-600" />
                Aulas
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2">
                {lessons.map((lesson, index) => {
                  const completed =
                    completedLessons.includes(lesson.id);

                  const active =
                    index === currentIndex;

                  return (
                    <button
                      key={lesson.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => goToLesson(index)}
                      title={lesson.title}
                      className={`flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-xs font-black transition ${
                        active
                          ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm"
                          : completed
                            ? "border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-100"
                            : "border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-500 hover:border-blue-200 hover:text-blue-600"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {completed && !active ? (
                        <Check size={14} />
                      ) : (
                        index + 1
                      )}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* MOBILE / TABLET LESSON NAVIGATION */}
          <div className="border-t border-slate-100 p-4 md:hidden">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => goToLesson(previousIndex)}
                disabled={currentIndex === 0}
                className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 shadow-sm transition hover:border-blue-200 hover:text-blue-600 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-35"
                aria-label="Aula anterior"
              >
                <ChevronLeft size={20} />
              </button>

              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-center">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <ListVideo size={16} className="text-blue-600" />

                  <span className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">
                    Aulas
                  </span>
                </div>

                <p className="mt-1 truncate text-sm font-black text-slate-950">
                  Aula {currentIndex + 1} de {lessons.length}
                </p>

                <p className="mt-0.5 truncate text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                  {currentLesson.title}
                </p>
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => goToLesson(nextIndex)}
                disabled={currentIndex === lessons.length - 1}
                className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 shadow-sm transition hover:border-blue-200 hover:text-blue-600 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-35"
                aria-label="Próxima aula"
              >
                <ChevronRight size={20} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
              {lessons.map((lesson, index) => {
                const completed =
                  completedLessons.includes(lesson.id);

                const active =
                  index === currentIndex;

                return (
                  <button
                    key={lesson.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => goToLesson(index)}
                    aria-label={`Ir para aula ${index + 1}`}
                    className={`h-1.5 rounded-full transition-all ${
                      active
                        ? "w-7 bg-blue-600"
                        : completed
                          ? "w-2 bg-emerald-400"
                          : "w-2 bg-slate-200"
                    }`}
                  />
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* LESSON INFORMATION */}
        <section className="mt-4 rounded-[1.5rem] border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-6">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
            <div className="hidden shrink-0 rounded-2xl bg-blue-50 p-3 text-blue-600 sm:block">
              <CirclePlay size={22} />
            </div>

            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-blue-600">
                Conteúdo da aula
              </p>

              <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
                Assista ao vídeo e avance quando estiver preparado para
                concluir esta aula.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* ERROR */}
        {error && (
          <div className="mt-4 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-5 py-4 text-sm text-red-700">
            <AlertCircle
              className="mt-0.5 shrink-0"
              size={18}
            />

            <span>{error}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* PREVIOUS / NEXT */}
        <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={previousLesson}
            disabled={currentIndex === 0 || saving}
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 shadow-sm transition hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={17} />
            Aula anterior
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={nextLesson}
            disabled={saving}
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-sm transition hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
          >
            {saving
              ? "A guardar..."
              : currentIndex === lessons.length - 1
                ? "Concluir e fazer teste"
                : "Concluir e próxima aula"}

            {currentIndex === lessons.length - 1 ? (
              <CheckCircle2 size={17} />
            ) : (
              <ArrowRight size={17} />
            )}
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* FINAL TEST MESSAGE */}
        <section className="mt-5 flex items-start gap-4 rounded-3xl border border-blue-100 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-50 to-indigo-50 p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-3 text-blue-600 shadow-sm">
            <CirclePlay size={22} />
          </div>

          <div>
            <p className="font-black text-slate-950">
              Próximo passo
            </p>

            <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
              {currentIndex === lessons.length - 1
                ? "Esta é a última aula. Ao concluí-la, poderá realizar o teste final."
                : `Faltam ${
                    lessons.length - completedCount
                  } aula${
                    lessons.length - completedCount !== 1
                      ? "s"
                      : ""
                  } para chegar ao teste final.`}
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

==========================================================================================
FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\pages\student\CoursesPage.jsx
==========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowRight,
  BookOpen,
  CheckCircle2,
  CirclePlay,
  Clock3,
  GraduationCap,
  ShieldCheck,
  Sparkles,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { courseService } from "../../services/api";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext";

const levels = [
  "Básico",
  "Básico / Intermédio",
  "Intermédio",
  "Intermédio",
  "Avançado",
];

const courseImages = [
  "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1563013544-824ae1b704d3?auto=format&fit=crop&w=900&q=85",
  "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1614064641938-3bbee52942c7?auto=format&fit=crop&w=900&q=85",
  "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1563986768609-322da13575f3?auto=format&fit=crop&w=900&q=85",
  "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1550751827-4bd374c3f58b?auto=format&fit=crop&w=900&q=85",
  "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1563013544-824ae1b704d3?auto=format&fit=crop&w=900&q=85",
];

export default function CoursesPage() {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [courses, setCourses] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    async function loadCourses() {
      try {
        setLoading(true);
        setError("");

        const data = await courseService.getCourses();

        if (active) {
          setCourses(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        if (active) {
          setError(
            err.message || "Não foi possível carregar os cursos."
          );
        }
      } finally {
        if (active) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    loadCourses();

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const completed = courses.filter((course) =>
      user?.completed_courses?.includes(course.id)
    ).length;

    const enrolled = courses.filter((course) =>
      user?.enrolled_courses?.includes(course.id)
    ).length;

    const lessons = courses.reduce(
      (total, course) =>
        total +
        Number(course.lessons_count || course.lessons?.length || 0),
      0
    );

    return {
      completed,
      enrolled,
      lessons,
    };
  }, [courses, user]);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl animate-pulse">
          <div className="h-52 rounded-[2rem] bg-white" />

          <div className="mt-5 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
            {[1, 2, 3].map((item) => (
              <div
                key={item}
                className="h-24 rounded-2xl bg-white"
              />
            ))}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 space-y-3">
            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((item) => (
              <div
                key={item}
                className="h-40 rounded-3xl bg-white"
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl">

        {/* HERO */}
        <section className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-[2rem] border border-blue-100 bg-white shadow-sm">
          <div className="absolute -right-24 -top-32 h-80 w-80 rounded-full bg-blue-100/60 blur-3xl" />
          <div className="absolute bottom-0 right-72 h-40 w-40 rounded-full bg-indigo-100/50 blur-3xl" />

          <div className="relative grid gap-8 px-6 py-8 sm:px-8 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_300px] lg:px-10 lg:py-9">
            <div className="max-w-3xl">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-blue-100 bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-blue-700">
                <Sparkles size={14} />
                Formação SICSI III
              </div>

              <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-black tracking-tight text-slate-950 sm:text-4xl">
                Aprenda a proteger
                <span className="block text-blue-600">
                  o seu mundo digital.
                </span>
              </h1>

              <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600 sm:text-base">
                Desenvolva competências práticas para reconhecer ameaças,
                proteger as suas contas e tomar decisões mais seguras no
                ambiente digital.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="hidden rounded-[1.75rem] border border-blue-100 bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-50 to-indigo-50 p-6 lg:block">
              <ShieldCheck className="h-10 w-10 text-blue-600" />

              <p className="mt-4 text-lg font-black text-slate-950">
                Aprenda.
              </p>

              <p className="text-lg font-black text-blue-600">
                Pratique.
              </p>

              <p className="text-lg font-black text-slate-950">
                Proteja.
              </p>

              <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                Complete as aulas e faça o teste final para conquistar o
                seu certificado.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* STATS */}
        <div className="mt-5 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-blue-50 p-3 text-blue-600">
                <BookOpen size={21} />
              </div>

              <div>
                <p className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
                  {courses.length}
                </p>

                <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">
                  Cursos disponíveis
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-indigo-50 p-3 text-indigo-600">
                <CirclePlay size={21} />
              </div>

              <div>
                <p className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
                  {stats.lessons}
                </p>

                <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">
                  Aulas disponíveis
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 p-3 text-emerald-600">
                <GraduationCap size={21} />
              </div>

              <div>
                <p className="text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
                  {stats.completed}
                </p>

                <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">
                  Cursos concluídos
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-5 py-4 text-sm font-medium text-red-700">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* COURSES */}
        <section className="mt-8">
          <div className="mb-5 flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-blue-600">
                Percurso de aprendizagem
              </p>

              <h2 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black tracking-tight text-slate-950">
                Os seus cursos
              </h2>
            </div>

            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
              {stats.enrolled} curso
              {stats.enrolled !== 1 ? "s" : ""} iniciado
              {stats.enrolled !== 1 ? "s" : ""}
            </p>
          </div>

          {courses.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white p-12 text-center">
              <BookOpen className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-slate-300" />

              <h2 className="mt-4 text-xl font-bold text-slate-900">
                Ainda não existem cursos publicados
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
                Os cursos disponibilizados pela SICSI aparecerão aqui.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {courses.map((course, index) => {
                const enrolled =
                  user?.enrolled_courses?.includes(course.id);

                const completed =
                  user?.completed_courses?.includes(course.id);

                const lessonCount = Number(
                  course.lessons_count ||
                    course.lessons?.length ||
                    10
                );

                const completedLessonIds =
                  user?.completed_lessons?.[course.id] || [];

                const completedLessonsCount = completed
                  ? lessonCount
                  : completedLessonIds.filter((id) =>
                      course.lessons?.some(
                        (lesson) => lesson.id === id
                      )
                    ).length;

                const progress = lessonCount
                  ? Math.round(
                      (completedLessonsCount / lessonCount) * 100
                    )
                  : 0;

                const image =
                  course.image_url ||
                  course.image ||
                  courseImages[index % courseImages.length];

                return (
                  <Link
                    key={course.id}
                    to={`/student/cursos/${course.id}`}
                    className="group block overflow-hidden rounded-[1.5rem] border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm transition duration-300 hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-blue-200 hover:shadow-xl"
                  >
                    <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row">
