                      {/* COURSE IMAGE */}
                      <div className="relative h-48 shrink-0 overflow-hidden md:h-auto md:w-56 lg:w-64">
                        <img
                          src={image}
                          alt=""
                          className="h-full w-full object-cover transition duration-500 group-hover:scale-105"
                          loading="lazy"
                        />

                        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-slate-950/70 via-slate-950/10 to-transparent" />

                        <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-4">
                          <span className="rounded-full bg-white/95 px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-700 shadow-sm">
                            {levels[index] || "Intermédio"}
                          </span>
                        </div>

                        {completed && (
                          <div className="absolute right-4 top-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-black text-white shadow-lg">
                            <CheckCircle2 size={13} />
                            Concluído
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>

                      {/* COURSE INFORMATION */}
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 p-5 sm:p-6">
                        <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-center">

                          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                            <h3 className="text-xl font-black tracking-tight text-slate-950 transition group-hover:text-blue-700">
                              {course.title}
                            </h3>

                            <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-2 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                              {course.description}
                            </p>

                            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">
                              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                <BookOpen size={14} />
                                {lessonCount} aulas
                              </span>

                              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                <Clock3 size={14} />
                                Teste final
                              </span>

                              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                <GraduationCap size={14} />
                                Certificado ≥70%
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          </div>

                          {/* PROGRESS */}
                          <div className="w-full shrink-0 lg:w-56">
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                              <span className="font-bold text-slate-500">
                                {progress}% concluído
                              </span>

                              <span className="font-bold text-blue-600">
                                {completed
                                  ? "Finalizado"
                                  : enrolled
                                    ? "Em progresso"
                                    : "Novo"}
                              </span>
                            </div>

                            <div className="mt-2 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-100">
                              <div
                                className="h-full rounded-full bg-blue-600 transition-all duration-500"
                                style={{
                                  width: `${progress}%`,
                                }}
                              />
                            </div>

                            <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between">
                              <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                                {completedLessonsCount}/{lessonCount} aulas
                              </span>

                              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white shadow-sm transition group-hover:bg-blue-700">
                                {completed
                                  ? "Rever"
                                  : enrolled
                                    ? "Continuar"
                                    : "Começar"}

                                <ArrowRight size={15} />
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="h-1 w-0 bg-blue-600 transition-all duration-300 group-hover:w-full" />
                  </Link>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </section>

        {/* CERTIFICATE */}
        <section className="mt-8 overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-blue-200 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-50 to-indigo-50">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:p-6">
            <div className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-white text-blue-600 shadow-sm">
              <GraduationCap size={24} />
            </div>

            <div>
              <h3 className="font-black text-slate-950">
                Complete um curso e conquiste o seu certificado.
              </h3>

              <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-5 text-slate-600">
                Conclua todas as aulas e obtenha pelo menos 70% no teste
                final. O certificado é gerado automaticamente.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

==========================================================================================
FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\pages\student\CourseTestPage.jsx
==========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  AlertCircle,
  ArrowLeft,
  ArrowRight,
  Award,
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  Circle,
  Lightbulb,
  RotateCcw,
  ShieldCheck,
  XCircle,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { courseService } from "../../services/api";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext";

export default function CourseTestPage() {
  const { courseId } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [course, setCourse] = useState(null);
  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState({});
  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(0);
  const [result, setResult] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    async function loadCourse() {
      try {
        setLoading(true);
        setError("");

        const data = await courseService.getCourse(courseId);

        if (active) {
          setCourse(data);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        if (active) {
          setError(err.message || "Não foi possível carregar o teste.");
        }
      } finally {
        if (active) {
          setLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    loadCourse();

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [courseId]);

  const questions = useMemo(
    () => (Array.isArray(course?.questions) ? course.questions : []),
    [course]
  );

  const currentQuestion = questions[currentIndex] || null;

  const answeredCount = Object.keys(answers).length;
  const progress = questions.length
    ? Math.round((answeredCount / questions.length) * 100)
    : 0;

  const currentAnswered =
    currentQuestion && Boolean(answers[currentQuestion.id]);

  function selectAnswer(optionId) {
    if (!currentQuestion || submitting) {
      return;
    }

    setAnswers((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [currentQuestion.id]: optionId,
    }));

    setError("");
  }

  function goPrevious() {
    if (currentIndex > 0) {
      setCurrentIndex((index) => index - 1);
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
    }
  }

  function goNext() {
    if (currentIndex < questions.length - 1) {
      setCurrentIndex((index) => index + 1);
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
    }
  }

  async function submit() {
    if (answeredCount !== questions.length) {
      setError(
        `Ainda faltam ${questions.length - answeredCount} pergunta${
          questions.length - answeredCount !== 1 ? "s" : ""
        }. Responda a todas antes de concluir.`
      );
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const orderedAnswers = questions.map(
        (question) => answers[question.id]
      );

      const data = await courseService.submitTest(courseId, {
        user_id: user.id,
        answers: orderedAnswers,
      });

      setResult(data);
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Não foi possível avaliar o teste.");
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-[70vh] bg-slate-50 px-4 py-8 sm:px-6">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl animate-pulse">
          <div className="h-5 w-40 rounded bg-slate-200" />
          <div className="mt-6 h-28 rounded-3xl bg-white" />
          <div className="mt-6 h-[420px] rounded-3xl bg-white" />
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error && !course) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-[70vh] bg-slate-50 px-4 py-10 sm:px-6">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl rounded-3xl border border-red-200 bg-white p-8 text-center shadow-sm">
          <AlertCircle className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-red-500" />
          <h1 className="mt-4 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
            Não foi possível carregar o teste
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">{error}</p>
          <Link
            to={`/student/cursos/${courseId}`}
            className="mt-6 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white"
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={16} />
            Voltar ao curso
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!course || questions.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-[70vh] bg-slate-50 px-4 py-10 sm:px-6">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-8 text-center shadow-sm">
          <ShieldCheck className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-blue-600" />
          <h1 className="mt-4 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
            Teste indisponível
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Este curso ainda não possui perguntas para o teste final.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (result) {
    const passed = Boolean(result.passed);

    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl">
          <div
            className={`overflow-hidden rounded-[2rem] border bg-white shadow-sm ${
              passed ? "border-emerald-200" : "border-amber-200"
            }`}
          >
            <div
              className={`px-6 py-10 text-center sm:px-10 ${
                passed
                  ? "bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-50 to-blue-50"
                  : "bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-50 to-blue-50"
              }`}
            >
              <div
                className={`mx-auto flex h-20 w-20 items-center justify-center rounded-3xl shadow-sm ${
                  passed
                    ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600"
                    : "bg-amber-100 text-amber-600"
                }`}
              >
                {passed ? <Award size={42} /> : <RotateCcw size={38} />}
              </div>

              <p className="mt-5 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-blue-600">
                Resultado do teste final
              </p>

              <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-black tracking-tight text-slate-950 sm:text-4xl">
                {passed
                  ? "Parabéns, concluiu o curso!"
                  : "Ainda não atingiu os 70%"}
              </h1>

              <p className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
                {result.message}
              </p>

              <div className="mx-auto mt-7 flex h-32 w-32 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white shadow-sm ring-8 ring-white/60">
                <div className="text-center">
                  <p className="text-4xl font-black text-slate-950">
                    {result.score}%
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                    resultado
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="p-6 sm:p-8">
              {result.certificate && (
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 rounded-2xl border border-blue-100 bg-blue-50/70 p-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                    <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-3 text-blue-600 shadow-sm">
                      <Award size={23} />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-blue-600">
                        Certificado gerado
                      </p>
                      <h2 className="mt-1 font-black text-slate-950">
                        {result.certificate.course_title}
                      </h2>
                      <p className="mt-1 font-mono text-xs text-slate-500">
                        {result.certificate.certificate_code}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <Link
                    to="/student/certificados"
                    className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-sm hover:bg-blue-700"
                  >
                    Ver certificado
                    <ArrowRight size={16} />
                  </Link>
                </div>
              )}

              {!passed && (
                <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-5">
                  <p className="font-bold text-amber-900">
                    O que fazer agora?
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm leading-6 text-amber-800">
                    Reveja as aulas, consulte o feedback abaixo e tente
                    novamente. O objectivo é aprender, não apenas passar.
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="mt-8">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-blue-600">
                      Revisão
                    </p>
                    <h2 className="mt-1 text-xl font-black text-slate-950">
                      Feedback das respostas
                    </h2>
                  </div>

                  <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-500">
                    {result.feedback?.filter((item) => item.correct).length || 0}/
                    {result.feedback?.length || questions.length} correctas
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-5 space-y-3">
                  {(result.feedback || []).map((item, index) => (
                    <div
                      key={item.question_id}
                      className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5"
                    >
                      <div className="flex gap-4">
                        <div
                          className={`mt-0.5 flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl ${
                            item.correct
                              ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600"
                              : "bg-red-50 text-red-500"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {item.correct ? (
                            <Check size={18} />
                          ) : (
                            <XCircle size={18} />
                          )}
                        </div>

                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <p className="text-sm font-black text-slate-900">
                            Pergunta {index + 1}
                          </p>
                          <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                            {item.correct
                              ? "Resposta correcta."
                              : "Resposta incorrecta."}
                          </p>
                          <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                            {item.explanation}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
                <Link
                  to={`/student/cursos/${courseId}`}
                  className="inline-flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50"
                >
                  <ArrowLeft size={16} />
                  Voltar ao curso
                </Link>

                {!passed && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setAnswers({});
                      setResult(null);
                      setCurrentIndex(0);
                      setError("");
                      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
                    }}
                    className="inline-flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-blue-700"
                  >
                    <RotateCcw size={16} />
                    Tentar novamente
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 px-4 py-6 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl">
        <Link
          to={`/student/cursos/${courseId}`}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500 transition hover:text-blue-600"
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={16} />
          Voltar ao curso
        </Link>

        <section className="mt-5 overflow-hidden rounded-[1.75rem] border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 p-5 sm:p-6 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
            <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-4">
              <div className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20">
                <ShieldCheck size={25} />
              </div>

              <div className="min-w-0">
                <p className="truncate text-sm font-black text-slate-950 sm:text-base">
                  {course.title}
                </p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">
                  Teste final · {questions.length} perguntas · aprovação ≥70%
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-blue-700">
              {answeredCount}/{questions.length} respondidas
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="border-t border-slate-100 px-5 py-4 sm:px-6">
            <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
              <span className="font-bold text-slate-700">
                Progresso do teste
              </span>
              <span className="font-bold text-blue-600">{progress}%</span>
            </div>

            <div className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-100">
              <div
                className="h-full rounded-full bg-blue-600 transition-all duration-300"
                style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {error && (
          <div className="mt-5 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-5 py-4 text-sm text-amber-900">
            <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-amber-600" size={19} />
            <span>{error}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        <main className="mt-5">
          <div className="rounded-[1.75rem] border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-black text-blue-700">
                <span>Pergunta {currentIndex + 1}</span>
                <span className="text-blue-300">/</span>
                <span>{questions.length}</span>
              </div>

              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400">
                {currentAnswered ? "Respondida" : "Por responder"}
              </span>
            </div>

            <h1 className="mt-7 max-w-3xl text-2xl font-black leading-tight tracking-tight text-slate-950 sm:text-3xl">
              {currentQuestion.question}
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-500">
              Seleccione a resposta que considera mais segura.
            </p>

            <div className="mt-7 space-y-3">
              {currentQuestion.options.map((option, optionIndex) => {
                const selected = answers[currentQuestion.id] === option.id;
                const letter = String.fromCharCode(65 + optionIndex);

                return (
                  <button
                    key={option.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => selectAnswer(option.id)}
                    className={`group flex w-full items-center gap-4 rounded-2xl border p-4 text-left transition duration-200 sm:p-5 ${
                      selected
                        ? "border-blue-500 bg-blue-50 shadow-sm"
                        : "border-slate-200 bg-white hover:border-blue-200 hover:bg-slate-50"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span
                      className={`flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl text-sm font-black ${
                        selected
                          ? "bg-blue-600 text-white"
                          : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 group-hover:bg-blue-50 group-hover:text-blue-600"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {selected ? <Check size={18} /> : letter}
                    </span>

                    <span
                      className={`flex-1 text-sm font-semibold leading-6 ${
                        selected ? "text-blue-950" : "text-slate-700"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {option.text}
                    </span>

                    <Circle
                      size={20}
                      className={
                        selected ? "text-blue-600" : "text-slate-200"
                      }
                      fill={selected ? "currentColor" : "none"}
                    />
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            <div className="mt-8 flex flex-col gap-3 border-t border-slate-100 pt-6 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={goPrevious}
                disabled={currentIndex === 0}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 transition hover:bg-slate-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
              >
                <ArrowLeft size={17} />
                Anterior
              </button>

              {currentIndex < questions.length - 1 ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={goNext}
                  className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-sm transition hover:bg-blue-700"
                >
                  Seguinte
                  <ArrowRight size={17} />
                </button>
              ) : (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={submit}
                  disabled={submitting}
                  className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-sm transition hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
                >
                  {submitting ? "A avaliar..." : "Concluir teste"}
                  <CheckCircle2 size={17} />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
            {questions.map((question, index) => {
              const answered = Boolean(answers[question.id]);
              const active = index === currentIndex;

              return (
                <button
                  key={question.id}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setCurrentIndex(index);
                    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
                  }}
                  aria-label={`Ir para pergunta ${index + 1}`}
                  className={`flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs font-black transition ${
                    active
                      ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-md"
                      : answered
                        ? "border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700"
                        : "border border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-500 hover:border-blue-200"
                  }`}
                >
                  {index + 1}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 flex items-start gap-4 rounded-2xl border border-blue-100 bg-blue-50 p-5">
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-2.5 text-blue-600 shadow-sm">
              <Lightbulb size={21} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-black text-slate-950">Dica de segurança</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-600">
                Em segurança digital, pare, verifique e só depois tome uma
                decisão. A pressa é frequentemente explorada por atacantes.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

==========================================================================================
FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\pages\student\SettingsPage.jsx
==========================================================================================
import { useState } from "react";
import {
  User,
  Mail,
  Lock,
  Save,
  ShieldCheck,
} from "lucide-react";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext";

export default function SettingsPage() {
  const { user, updateUser } =
    useAuth();

  const [name, setName] =
    useState(user?.name || "");

  const [email, setEmail] =
    useState(user?.email || "");

  const [password, setPassword] =
    useState("");

  const [message, setMessage] =
    useState("");

  function saveProfile(event) {
    event.preventDefault();

    if (!name.trim()) {
      setMessage(
        "Informe o seu nome."
      );
      return;
    }

    const updated = {
      ...user,
      name: name.trim(),
      email: email.trim(),
    };

    updateUser(updated);

    localStorage.setItem(
      "sicsi_user",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setMessage(
      "Dados da conta atualizados nesta sessão."
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-5 sm:p-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl">
        <p className="text-sm text-blue-400">
          Conta
        </p>

        <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black">
          Definições
        </h1>

        <p className="mt-2 text-slate-500">
          Consulte e atualize os seus dados pessoais.
        </p>

        <div className="mt-8 grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_300px]">
          <form
            onSubmit={saveProfile}
            className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-blue-500/10 p-3 text-blue-500">
                <User size={22} />
              </div>

              <div>
                <h2 className="font-bold">
                  Dados pessoais
                </h2>

                <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                  Informação básica da sua conta.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-7 space-y-5">
              <Field
                label="Nome completo"
                icon={User}
                value={name}
                onChange={setName}
              />

              <Field
                label="Email"
                icon={Mail}
                type="email"
                value={email}
                onChange={setEmail}
              />

              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-300">
                  Nova palavra-passe
                </label>

                <div className="relative">
                  <Lock
                    size={17}
                    className="absolute left-3 top-3.5 text-slate-600"
                  />

                  <input
                    type="password"
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(event) =>
                      setPassword(
                        event.target.value
                      )
                    }
                    placeholder="Deixe vazio para manter"
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-10 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {message && (
              <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/10 p-4 text-sm text-blue-300">
                {message}
              </div>
            )}

            <button
              type="submit"
              className="mt-6 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-blue-500"
            >
              <Save size={17} />
              Guardar alterações
            </button>
          </form>

          <aside className="h-fit rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <ShieldCheck
                className="text-blue-500"
                size={25}
              />

              <h2 className="font-bold">
                Conta SICSI
              </h2>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-6 space-y-4">
              <Info
                label="Função"
                value={
                  user?.role === "admin"
                    ? "Administrador"
                    : "Estudante"
                }
              />

              <Info
                label="Nível"
                value={
                  user?.awareness_level ||
                  "Não avaliado"
                }
              />

              <Info
                label="Email"
                value={user?.email}
              />
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Field({
  label,
  icon: Icon,
  value,
  onChange,
  type = "text",
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-300">
        {label}
      </label>

      <div className="relative">
        <Icon
          size={17}
          className="absolute left-3 top-3.5 text-slate-600"
        />

        <input
          type={type}
          value={value}
          onChange={(event) =>
            onChange(event.target.value)
          }
          className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-10 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Info({ label, value }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-950 p-4">
      <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
        {label}
      </p>

      <p className="mt-1 truncate text-sm font-semibold">
        {value || "—"}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}


==========================================================================================
FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\pages\student\SimulationPage.jsx
==========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useParams, Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  ArrowRight,
  ShieldAlert,
  Mail,
  UserCheck,
  XCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertTriangle,
} from "lucide-react";
import { simulationService } from "../../services/api";

export default function SimulationPage() {
  const { simulationId } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [simulation, setSimulation] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [acting, setActing] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const user = JSON.parse(
    localStorage.getItem("sicsi_user") || "null"
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    setLoading(true);
    setResult(null);
    setError("");

    simulationService
      .get(simulationId)
      .then(setSimulation)
      .catch((err) => {
        setError(err.message);
      })
      .finally(() => {
        setLoading(false);
      });
  }, [simulationId]);

  async function act(action) {
    if (acting) return;

    setActing(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const data = await simulationService.evaluate(
        simulationId,
        action,
        user?.id
      );

      setResult(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setActing(false);
    }
  }

  function retry() {
    setResult(null);
    setError("");
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="p-5 sm:p-8">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-8 text-center">
            <div className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 animate-spin rounded-full border-4 border-blue-500 border-t-transparent" />
            <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-slate-500">
              A preparar a simulação...
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!simulation) {
    return (
      <div className="p-5 sm:p-8">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 p-6 text-red-300">
            {error || "Simulação não encontrada."}
          </div>

          <Link
            to="/student/simulacoes"
            className="mt-5 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-blue-400"
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={16} />
            Voltar às simulações
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const isPhishing =
    simulation.category === "Phishing";

  const isSocial =
    simulation.category === "Engenharia Social";

  const isPirated =
    simulation.category === "Software Pirateado";

  const isCredentials =
    simulation.category === "Contas e Credenciais";

  return (
    <div className="p-5 sm:p-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl">
        <Link
          to="/student/simulacoes"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-500 transition hover:text-white"
        >
          <ArrowLeft size={16} />
          Voltar às simulações
        </Link>

        <div className="mt-7">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-blue-400">
            {simulation.category}
          </p>

          <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black sm:text-4xl">
            {simulation.title}
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-2 text-slate-500">
            Observe cuidadosamente, analise os sinais e tome uma
            decisão.
          </p>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 p-4 text-sm text-red-300">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {isPhishing ? (
          <div className="mt-8 overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 shadow-2xl">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 px-5 py-4">
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-blue-500/10 p-2">
                <Mail className="text-blue-500" size={22} />
              </div>

              <div>
                <p className="font-bold">
                  Caixa de entrada
                </p>

                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                  Ambiente de aprendizagem simulado
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="p-6 sm:p-8">
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950">
                <div className="border-b border-slate-800 p-5">
                  <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">
                    Remetente
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-2 break-all font-semibold text-slate-200">
                    {simulation.sender}
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-5 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">
                    Assunto
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-2 text-lg font-bold text-white">
                    {simulation.subject}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="p-5 sm:p-7">
                  <p className="max-w-3xl whitespace-pre-line leading-7 text-slate-300">
                    {simulation.message}
                  </p>

                  {simulation.link_text && (
                    <div className="mt-7">
                      <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">
                        Link apresentado
                      </p>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={acting || !!result}
                        onClick={() => act("click")}
                        className="mt-3 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
                      >
                        {simulation.link_text}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {simulation.link_target && (
                    <div className="mt-7">
                      <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">
                        Endereço apresentado pelo link
                      </p>

                      <div className="mt-2 break-all rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-4 font-mono text-xs text-slate-400">
                        {simulation.link_target}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="mt-8 rounded-3xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6 sm:p-8">
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-6 sm:p-8">
              <ShieldAlert
                size={38}
                className="text-blue-500"
              />

              <p className="mt-6 whitespace-pre-line text-lg leading-8 text-slate-300">
                {simulation.scenario ||
                  simulation.message ||
                  simulation.description}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {!result && (
          <div className="mt-6 rounded-3xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6 sm:p-8">
            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <ShieldAlert
                size={22}
                className="mt-0.5 text-blue-500"
              />

              <div>
                <h2 className="font-bold">
                  Qual é a sua decisão?
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                  Escolha a acção que considera mais segura.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-6 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
              {isPhishing && (
                <>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={acting}
                    onClick={() => act("inspect")}
                    className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 p-4 font-semibold transition hover:border-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-500/10 disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    <UserCheck size={18} />
                    Verificar cuidadosamente
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={acting}
                    onClick={() => act("report")}
                    className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 p-4 font-semibold transition hover:border-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-500/10 disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    <ShieldAlert size={18} />
                    Denunciar / reportar
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={acting}
                    onClick={() => act("click")}
                    className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/5 p-4 font-semibold text-red-300 transition hover:bg-red-500/10 disabled:opacity-50 sm:col-span-2"
                  >
                    Abrir o link
                  </button>
                </>
              )}

              {isSocial && (
                <>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={acting}
                    onClick={() => act("verify")}
                    className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 p-4 font-semibold hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    Verificar identidade
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={acting}
                    onClick={() => act("act")}
                    className="rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/5 p-4 font-semibold text-red-300 hover:bg-red-500/10 disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    Agir sem verificar
                  </button>
                </>
              )}

              {isPirated && (
                <>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={acting}
                    onClick={() => act("cancel")}
                    className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 p-4 font-semibold hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    Cancelar e procurar fonte oficial
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={acting}
                    onClick={() => act("install")}
                    className="rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/5 p-4 font-semibold text-red-300 hover:bg-red-500/10 disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    Instalar mesmo assim
                  </button>
                </>
              )}

              {isCredentials && (
                <>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={acting}
                    onClick={() => act("secure")}
                    className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 p-4 font-semibold hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    Proteger a conta
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    disabled={acting}
                    onClick={() => act("ignore")}
                    className="rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/5 p-4 font-semibold text-red-300 hover:bg-red-500/10 disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    Ignorar o alerta
                  </button>
                </>
              )}

              {!isPhishing &&
                !isSocial &&
                !isPirated &&
                !isCredentials && (
                  <>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      disabled={acting}
                      onClick={() => act("ignore")}
                      className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 p-4 font-semibold hover:bg-blue-500 disabled:opacity-50"
                    >
                      Verificar por canal oficial
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      disabled={acting}
                      onClick={() => act("act")}
                      className="rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/5 p-4 font-semibold text-red-300 hover:bg-red-500/10 disabled:opacity-50"
                    >
                      Prosseguir sem verificar
                    </button>
                  </>
                )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {result && (
          <div
            className={`mt-6 rounded-3xl border p-6 sm:p-8 ${
              result.correct
                ? "border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/5"
                : "border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/5"
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
              {result.correct ? (
                <CheckCircle2
                  size={38}
                  className="shrink-0 text-emerald-400"
                />
              ) : (
                <AlertTriangle
                  size={38}
                  className="shrink-0 text-amber-400"
                />
              )}

              <div className="min-w-0">
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-black">
                  {result.correct
                    ? "Boa decisão!"
                    : "Atenção à sua decisão"}
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-3 leading-7 text-slate-300">
                  {result.message ||
                    result.feedback ||
                    (result.correct
                      ? "A sua decisão foi segura."
                      : "Esta decisão apresenta riscos.")}
                </p>

                <div className="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/70 p-5">
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white">
                    Porque é importante?
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-2 leading-7 text-slate-400">
                    {result.explanation ||
                      "Analise sempre o contexto, confirme a origem da informação e evite agir por impulso."}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
                  {!result.correct && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={retry}
                      className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 font-bold hover:bg-blue-500"
                    >
                      Tentar novamente
                    </button>
                  )}

                  {result.correct && (
                    <Link
                      to="/student/simulacoes"
                      className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 font-bold hover:bg-blue-500"
                    >
                      Próxima simulação
                      <ArrowRight size={18} />
                    </Link>
                  )}

                  <Link
                    to="/student/simulacoes"
                    className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 px-5 py-3 font-semibold text-slate-300 hover:text-white"
                  >
                    Ver todas
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="mt-8 rounded-2xl border border-blue-500/10 bg-blue-500/5 p-5">
          <div className="flex gap-3">
            <ShieldAlert
              size={20}
              className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-blue-500"
            />

            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-200">
                Ambiente seguro
              </p>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                Esta é uma reprodução educativa. Nenhuma acção
                realizada nesta simulação afecta contas, emails,
                dispositivos ou serviços reais.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => navigate("/student/simulacoes")}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-500 hover:text-white"
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={16} />
            Voltar para a área de simulações
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


==========================================================================================
FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\pages\student\StudentDashboardPage.jsx
==========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  ArrowRight,
  Award,
  BookOpen,
  Check,
  CheckCircle2,
  ChevronRight,
  CirclePlay,
  ShieldAlert,
  ShieldCheck,
  Sparkles,
  Target,
  TrendingUp,
} from "lucide-react";
import { courseService } from "../../services/api";
import { useAuth } from "../../context/AuthContext";

export default function StudentDashboardPage() {
  const { user } = useAuth();

  const [courses, setCourses] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    courseService
      .getCourses()
      .then((data) => {
        if (active) {
          setCourses(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) setCourses([]);
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (active) setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, []);

  const enrolledCourses = useMemo(
    () =>
      courses.filter((course) =>
        user?.enrolled_courses?.includes(course.id)
      ),
    [courses, user?.enrolled_courses]
  );

  const completedCourseIds = user?.completed_courses || [];

  const completedCourses = completedCourseIds.length;

  const certificates = completedCourses;

  const simulationsCompleted = user?.simulation_results
    ? Object.keys(user.simulation_results).length
    : 0;

  const currentCourse =
    enrolledCourses.find(
      (course) => !completedCourseIds.includes(course.id)
    ) || enrolledCourses[0];

  function getProgress(course) {
    if (!course) return 0;

    const completed = user?.completed_lessons?.[course.id] || [];

    if (!course.lessons_count) return 0;

    return Math.min(
      100,
      Math.round((completed.length / course.lessons_count) * 100)
    );
  }

  const globalProgress = useMemo(() => {
    if (enrolledCourses.length === 0) return 0;

    const total = enrolledCourses.reduce(
      (sum, course) => sum + getProgress(course),
      0
    );

    return Math.round(total / enrolledCourses.length);
  }, [enrolledCourses, user?.completed_lessons]);

  const firstName = user?.name?.split(" ")[0] || "Estudante";

  const awarenessLevel = user?.awareness_level || "Ainda não avaliado";

  const assessmentScore =
    user?.assessment_score !== undefined &&
    user?.assessment_score !== null
      ? Number(user.assessment_score)
      : null;

  const recommendations = Array.isArray(user?.recommendations)
    ? user.recommendations
    : [];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-full bg-slate-50">
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-5 py-6 sm:px-8 lg:px-10 lg:py-9">
        {/* =========================================================
            CABEÇALHO
        ========================================================== */}
        <header className="mb-7 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-blue-600">
              Área do estudante
            </p>

            <h1 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-950 sm:text-3xl">
              Olá, {firstName}. <span aria-hidden="true">👋</span>
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500 sm:text-base">
              Continue a desenvolver as suas competências de segurança
              digital.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="hidden h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-blue-600 text-lg font-bold text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20 sm:flex">
            {firstName.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
          </div>
        </header>

        {/* =========================================================
            HERO
        ========================================================== */}
        <section className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-[2rem] border border-blue-100 bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-600 via-blue-700 to-blue-900 shadow-2xl shadow-blue-900/10">
          <div className="absolute -right-20 -top-24 h-72 w-72 rounded-full bg-white/10 blur-3xl" />
          <div className="absolute -bottom-32 left-1/3 h-80 w-80 rounded-full bg-sky-400/10 blur-3xl" />

          <div className="relative grid gap-8 p-6 sm:p-8 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_330px] lg:p-10">
            <div className="max-w-3xl">
              <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-white/15 bg-white/10 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-blue-50 backdrop-blur">
                <ShieldCheck className="h-4 w-4" />
                O seu espaço de aprendizagem
              </div>

              <h2 className="mt-6 max-w-2xl text-3xl font-bold leading-tight tracking-tight text-white sm:text-4xl lg:text-5xl">
                Conhecimento é a primeira camada de defesa.
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-5 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-7 text-blue-100 sm:text-base">
                Aprenda, pratique e desenvolva hábitos mais seguros para
                enfrentar os riscos do mundo digital.
              </p>

              <div className="mt-7 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
                <Link
                  to="/student/cursos"
                  className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-white px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-bold text-blue-700 shadow-xl shadow-blue-950/20 transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:bg-blue-50"
                >
                  Explorar cursos
                  <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                </Link>

                <Link
                  to="/student/simulacoes"
                  className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-white/20 bg-white/10 px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white backdrop-blur transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:bg-white/15"
                >
                  Praticar simulações
                  <ShieldAlert className="h-4 w-4" />
                </Link>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-3xl border border-white/15 bg-white/10 p-5 backdrop-blur-md lg:self-end">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-blue-100">
                    Consciência digital
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-white">
                    {awarenessLevel}
                  </p>

                  {assessmentScore !== null && (
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-medium text-blue-100">
                      Avaliação inicial: {assessmentScore}%
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-white/10 ring-1 ring-white/15">
                  <Target className="h-7 w-7 text-white" />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-6 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/15">
                <div
                  className="h-full rounded-full bg-white transition-all duration-700"
                  style={{
                    width: `${Math.max(
                      0,
                      Math.min(100, assessmentScore ?? 0)
                    )}%`,
                  }}
                />
              </div>

              <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-blue-100">
                O seu percurso é orientado pelo seu nível de consciência
                digital.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* =========================================================
            ESTATÍSTICAS
        ========================================================== */}
        <section className="mt-6 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
          <StatCard
            icon={BookOpen}
            label="Cursos inscritos"
            value={enrolledCourses.length}
            helper="No seu percurso"
          />

          <StatCard
            icon={CirclePlay}
            label="Cursos concluídos"
            value={completedCourses}
            helper="Aprendizagem concluída"
          />

          <StatCard
            icon={ShieldAlert}
            label="Simulações"
            value={simulationsCompleted}
            helper="Experiências realizadas"
          />

          <StatCard
            icon={Award}
            label="Certificados"
            value={certificates}
            helper="Conquistas obtidas"
          />
        </section>

        {/* =========================================================
            PROGRESSO GLOBAL
        ========================================================== */}
        <section className="mt-6 rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-lg shadow-slate-200/40 sm:p-7">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                <TrendingUp className="h-6 w-6" />
              </div>

              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-blue-600">
                  Percurso de aprendizagem
                </p>

                <h2 className="mt-1 text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
                  O seu progresso global
                </h2>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="text-left sm:text-right">
              <p className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-950">
                {globalProgress}%
              </p>

              <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">
                progresso médio dos cursos
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 h-3 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-100">
            <div
              className="h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-500 to-blue-700 transition-all duration-700"
              style={{ width: `${globalProgress}%` }}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">
            <span>
              {completedCourses} curso{completedCourses === 1 ? "" : "s"}{" "}
              concluído{completedCourses === 1 ? "" : "s"}
            </span>

            <span>
              {enrolledCourses.length} inscrito
              {enrolledCourses.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}
            </span>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* =========================================================
            CONTEÚDO PRINCIPAL
        ========================================================== */}
        <div className="mt-6 grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_360px]">
          {/* CURSO ACTUAL */}
          <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-lg shadow-slate-200/40">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-b border-slate-100 p-6 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between sm:p-7">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.15em] text-blue-600">
                  Aprendizagem
                </p>

                <h2 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-950">
                  Continue a aprender
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
                  Retome o seu percurso exactamente onde ficou.
                </p>
              </div>

              <Link
                to="/student/cursos"
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold text-blue-600 transition hover:text-blue-700"
              >
                Ver cursos
                <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" />
              </Link>
            </div>

            <div className="p-6 sm:p-7">
              {loading ? (
                <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-slate-50 p-8">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                    <div className="h-12 w-12 animate-pulse rounded-2xl bg-slate-200" />

                    <div className="flex-1">
                      <div className="h-4 w-2/3 animate-pulse rounded bg-slate-200" />
                      <div className="mt-3 h-3 w-1/2 animate-pulse rounded bg-slate-100" />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="mt-7 h-3 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-slate-200" />
                </div>
              ) : currentCourse ? (
                <div className="group rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 to-white p-5 transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-blue-200 hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-slate-200/60 sm:p-6">
                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between">
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                      <div className="flex h-14 w-14 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20">
                        <BookOpen className="h-7 w-7" />
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.12em] text-blue-600">
                          Curso actual
                        </p>

                        <h3 className="mt-1 text-xl font-bold leading-tight text-slate-950">
                          {currentCourse.title}
                        </h3>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-bold text-blue-700">
                      <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                      {getProgress(currentCourse)}%
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {currentCourse.description && (
                    <p className="mt-6 line-clamp-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                      {currentCourse.description}
                    </p>
                  )}

                  <div className="mt-6">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-bold">
                      <span className="text-slate-500">
                        Progresso do curso
                      </span>

                      <span className="text-blue-600">
                        {getProgress(currentCourse)}%
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-2 h-3 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-100">
                      <div
                        className="h-full rounded-full bg-blue-600 transition-all duration-700"
                        style={{
                          width: `${getProgress(currentCourse)}%`,
                        }}
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                    <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">
                      {currentCourse.lessons_count || 0} aulas disponíveis
                    </p>

                    <Link
                      to={`/student/cursos/${currentCourse.id}`}
                      className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20 transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:bg-blue-700 hover:shadow-xl"
                    >
                      Continuar aprendizagem
                      <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-6 py-12 text-center">
                  <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-white text-blue-600 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-slate-100">
                    <BookOpen className="h-7 w-7" />
                  </div>

                  <h3 className="mt-5 text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">
                    O seu percurso começa aqui.
                  </h3>

                  <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-md text-sm leading-6 text-slate-500">
                    Explore os cursos disponíveis e escolha o próximo passo
                    para desenvolver os seus conhecimentos.
                  </p>

                  <Link
                    to="/student/cursos"
                    className="mt-6 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20 transition hover:bg-blue-700"
                  >
                    Explorar cursos
                    <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </Link>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </section>

          {/* COLUNA LATERAL */}
          <aside className="space-y-6">
            {/* RECOMENDAÇÕES */}
            <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-lg shadow-slate-200/40 sm:p-7">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <div className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600">
                  <Sparkles className="h-5 w-5" />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-blue-600">
                    Personalizado
                  </p>

                  <h2 className="mt-1 text-xl font-bold text-slate-950">
                    Recomendado para si
                  </h2>
                </div>
              </div>

              {recommendations.length > 0 ? (
                <div className="mt-6 space-y-3">
                  {recommendations.slice(0, 4).map((recommendation, index) => (
                    <div
                      key={`${recommendation}-${index}`}
                      className="group flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-slate-50 p-4 transition-all hover:border-blue-100 hover:bg-blue-50/50"
                    >
                      <div className="mt-0.5 flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-sm">
                        <Check className="h-4 w-4" />
                      </div>

                      <p className="text-sm font-semibold leading-5 text-slate-700">
                        {recommendation}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-5">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                    As suas recomendações aparecerão aqui.
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500">
                    Complete a autoavaliação inicial para receber um percurso
                    de aprendizagem adaptado ao seu nível.
                  </p>

                  {!user?.initial_assessment_completed && (
                    <Link
                      to="/student/autoavaliacao"
                      className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-700"
                    >
                      Fazer autoavaliação
                      <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </Link>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </section>

            {/* ACÇÕES RÁPIDAS */}
            <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-lg shadow-slate-200/40 sm:p-7">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-blue-600">
                Acesso rápido
              </p>

              <h2 className="mt-1 text-xl font-bold text-slate-950">
                Continue a evoluir
              </h2>

              <div className="mt-5 space-y-3">
                <QuickAction
                  to="/student/simulacoes"
                  icon={ShieldAlert}
                  title="Praticar simulações"
                  description="Aprenda através de situações realistas."
                />

                <QuickAction
                  to="/student/certificados"
                  icon={Award}
                  title="Os meus certificados"
                  description="Consulte as suas conquistas."
                />

                <QuickAction
                  to="/student/cursos"
                  icon={BookOpen}
                  title="Explorar cursos"
                  description="Descubra novos conteúdos."
                />
              </div>
            </section>
          </aside>
        </div>

        {/* =========================================================
            MOTIVAÇÃO FINAL
        ========================================================== */}
        <section className="mt-6 overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-blue-100 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-50 via-white to-sky-50 p-6 sm:p-7">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
              <div className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20">
                <ShieldCheck className="h-5 w-5" />
              </div>

              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">
                  Segurança digital constrói-se com prática.
                </p>

                <p className="mt-1 max-w-2xl text-xs leading-5 text-slate-500 sm:text-sm">
                  Cada aula concluída e cada simulação realizada aproxima-o
                  de decisões mais seguras no mundo digital.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <Link
              to="/student/simulacoes"
              className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-blue-200 bg-white px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-blue-700 shadow-sm transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-blue-300 hover:shadow-md"
            >
              Praticar agora
              <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
            </Link>
          </div>
        </section>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}

function StatCard({ icon: Icon, label, value, helper }) {
  return (
    <article className="group rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-lg shadow-slate-200/30 transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-blue-100 hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-slate-200/60">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 transition-colors group-hover:bg-blue-600 group-hover:text-white">
          <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
        </div>

        <span className="text-3xl font-black tracking-tight text-slate-950">
          {value}
        </span>
      </div>

      <p className="mt-5 text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">{label}</p>

      <p className="mt-1 text-xs font-medium text-slate-400">{helper}</p>
    </article>
  );
}

function QuickAction({ to, icon: Icon, title, description }) {
  return (
    <Link
      to={to}
      className="group flex items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-slate-50 p-4 transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-blue-100 hover:bg-blue-50/50 hover:shadow-md"
    >
      <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-white text-blue-600 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-slate-100 transition-colors group-hover:bg-blue-600 group-hover:text-white">
        <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
      </div>

      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">{title}</p>

        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-slate-400">
          {description}
        </p>
      </div>

      <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-slate-300 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5 group-hover:text-blue-600" />
    </Link>
  );
}

==========================================================================================
FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\pages\student\StudentPlaceholders.jsx
==========================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  ArrowRight,
  ShieldAlert,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertTriangle,
  Mail,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { simulationService } from "../../services/api";

export function SimulationsPage() {
  const [simulations, setSimulations] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    simulationService
      .getAll()
      .then(setSimulations)
      .catch((err) => {
        setError(err.message);
      })
      .finally(() => {
        setLoading(false);
      });
  }, []);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="p-5 sm:p-8">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl">
          <p className="text-slate-500">
            A preparar simulações...
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-5 sm:p-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl">
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-blue-400">
          Aprendizagem prática
        </p>

        <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black sm:text-4xl">
          Simulações
        </h1>

        <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-slate-500">
          Pratique a identificação e resposta a situações
          reais de risco digital num ambiente totalmente seguro.
        </p>

        <div className="mt-8 flex items-start gap-4 rounded-2xl border border-blue-500/20 bg-blue-500/5 p-5">
          <ShieldAlert className="mt-1 shrink-0 text-blue-500" />

          <div>
            <h2 className="font-bold">
              Ambiente seguro de simulação
            </h2>

            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
              Todas as situações são fictícias. Nenhuma ação
              executada aqui afecta contas, emails ou serviços reais.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {error && (
          <div className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 p-4 text-sm text-red-300">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="mt-8 grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {simulations.map((simulation) => (
            <Link
              key={simulation.id}
              to={`/student/simulacoes/${simulation.id}`}
              className="group rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6 transition hover:-translate-y-1 hover:border-blue-500/50"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                <div className="rounded-xl bg-blue-500/10 p-3 text-blue-500">
                  {simulation.category === "Phishing" ? (
                    <Mail size={24} />
                  ) : (
                    <ShieldAlert size={24} />
                  )}
                </div>

                <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-800 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-slate-400">
                  {simulation.difficulty}
                </span>
              </div>

              <p className="mt-6 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-blue-400">
                {simulation.category}
              </p>

              <h2 className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold">
                {simulation.title}
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-3 line-clamp-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
                {simulation.subject ||
                  simulation.scenario ||
                  simulation.message}
              </p>
