              <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-400">
                  Iniciar cenário
                </span>

                <ArrowRight
                  size={18}
                  className="text-blue-500 transition group-hover:translate-x-1"
                />
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function SimulationPlayerPage({ simulationId }) {
  const [simulation, setSimulation] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [selectedAction, setSelectedAction] = useState("");
  const [result, setResult] = useState(null);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const user = JSON.parse(
    localStorage.getItem("sicsi_user") || "null"
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    simulationService
      .get(simulationId)
      .then(setSimulation)
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [simulationId]);

  async function evaluate(action) {
    setSelectedAction(action);
    setSubmitting(true);

    try {
      const data = await simulationService.evaluate(
        simulationId,
        action,
        user?.id
      );

      setResult(data);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="p-8 text-slate-500">
        A preparar o cenário...
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!simulation) {
    return (
      <div className="p-8 text-red-300">
        Simulação não encontrada.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const isPhishing = simulation.category === "Phishing";

  return (
    <div className="p-5 sm:p-8">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl">
        <Link
          to="/student/simulacoes"
          className="text-sm font-semibold text-blue-400 hover:text-blue-300"
        >
          ← Voltar às simulações
        </Link>

        <div className="mt-6">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-400">
            {simulation.category}
          </p>

          <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-black">
            {simulation.title}
          </h1>
        </div>

        {isPhishing ? (
          <div className="mt-8 overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900">
            <div className="border-b border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 px-5 py-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <Mail className="text-blue-500" />

                <div>
                  <p className="font-bold">
                    Caixa de entrada
                  </p>

                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                    Ambiente simulado
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="p-6 sm:p-8">
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-5">
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
                  De
                </p>

                <p className="mt-1 font-semibold">
                  {simulation.sender}
                </p>

                <p className="mt-5 text-sm text-slate-500">
                  Assunto
                </p>

                <p className="mt-1 font-bold">
                  {simulation.subject}
                </p>

                <div className="my-6 h-px bg-slate-800" />

                <p className="leading-7 text-slate-300">
                  {simulation.message}
                </p>

                <div className="mt-6 rounded-xl border border-blue-500/20 bg-blue-500/5 p-4">
                  <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-blue-400">
                    Link da mensagem
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-2 break-all text-sm text-blue-300">
                    {simulation.link_target}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {!result && (
                <div className="mt-8">
                  <h2 className="font-bold">
                    O que faria nesta situação?
                  </h2>

                  <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
                    <button
                      disabled={submitting}
                      onClick={() => evaluate("inspect")}
                      className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 p-4 text-left hover:border-blue-500"
                    >
                      <strong>Inspeccionar</strong>
                      <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-slate-500">
                        Analisar remetente e link.
                      </span>
                    </button>

                    <button
                      disabled={submitting}
                      onClick={() => evaluate("report")}
                      className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 p-4 text-left hover:border-blue-500"
                    >
                      <strong>Denunciar</strong>
                      <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-slate-500">
                        Marcar a mensagem como suspeita.
                      </span>
                    </button>

                    <button
                      disabled={submitting}
                      onClick={() => evaluate("open")}
                      className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 p-4 text-left hover:border-red-500"
                    >
                      <strong>Abrir link</strong>
                      <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-slate-500">
                        Prosseguir para o endereço.
                      </span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {result && (
                <div
                  className={`mt-8 rounded-2xl border p-6 ${
                    result.correct
                      ? "border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10"
                      : "border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10"
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                    {result.correct ? (
                      <CheckCircle2 className="shrink-0 text-emerald-400" />
                    ) : (
                      <AlertTriangle className="shrink-0 text-amber-400" />
                    )}

                    <div>
                      <h2 className="font-bold">
                        {result.correct
                          ? "Boa decisão!"
                          : "Atenção à sua decisão"}
                      </h2>

                      <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-300">
                        {result.feedback}
                      </p>

                      <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-950/60 p-4">
                        <p className="text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
                          {result.explanation}
                        </p>
                      </div>

                      {!result.correct && (
                        <button
                          onClick={() => {
                            setResult(null);
                            setSelectedAction("");
                          }}
                          className="mt-5 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold hover:bg-blue-500"
                        >
                          Tentar novamente
                        </button>
                      )}

                      {result.correct && (
                        <Link
                          to="/student/simulacoes"
                          className="mt-5 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold hover:bg-blue-500"
                        >
                          Próxima simulação
                          <ArrowRight size={17} />
                        </Link>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="mt-8 rounded-3xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900 p-6 sm:p-8">
            <div className="rounded-2xl bg-slate-950 p-6">
              <p className="leading-7 text-slate-300">
                {simulation.scenario}
              </p>
            </div>

            {!result && (
              <div className="mt-7">
                <h2 className="font-bold">
                  Qual seria a sua decisão?
                </h2>

                <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => evaluate(simulation.safe_action)}
                    className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 p-4 text-left hover:border-blue-500"
                  >
                    Tomar uma decisão segura
                  </button>

                  <button
                    onClick={() => evaluate("act")}
                    className="rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 p-4 text-left hover:border-red-500"
                  >
                    Prosseguir sem verificar
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {result && (
              <div className="mt-7 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 p-6">
                <h2 className="font-bold">
                  {result.correct
                    ? "Boa decisão!"
                    : "Atenção"}
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
                  {result.feedback}
                </p>

                <p className="mt-4 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-400">
                  {result.explanation}
                </p>

                {result.correct && (
                  <Link
                    to="/student/simulacoes"
                    className="mt-5 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold"
                  >
                    Próxima simulação
                    <ArrowRight size={17} />
                  </Link>
                )}

                {!result.correct && (
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setResult(null)}
                    className="mt-5 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold"
                  >
                    Tentar novamente
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


==========================================================================================
FRONTEND SOURCE — frontend\src\services\api.js
==========================================================================================
const API_URL = "http://localhost:8000/api";

async function request(endpoint, options = {}) {
  const response = await fetch(
    `${API_URL}${endpoint}`,
    {
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        ...(options.headers || {}),
      },
      ...options,
    }
  );

  const data = await response
    .json()
    .catch(() => ({}));

  if (!response.ok) {
    let message = "Ocorreu um erro no servidor.";

    if (typeof data.detail === "string") {
      message = data.detail;
    } else if (Array.isArray(data.detail)) {
      message = data.detail
        .map((item) => {
          if (typeof item === "string") {
            return item;
          }

          if (item?.msg) {
            return item.msg;
          }

          return JSON.stringify(item);
        })
        .join(" | ");
    } else if (
      data.detail &&
      typeof data.detail === "object"
    ) {
      message =
        data.detail.msg ||
        data.detail.message ||
        JSON.stringify(data.detail);
    } else if (data.message) {
      message =
        typeof data.message === "string"
          ? data.message
          : JSON.stringify(data.message);
    }

    throw new Error(message);
  }

  return data;
}

export const authService = {
  register: (payload) =>
    request("/auth/register", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    }),

  login: (payload) =>

      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    }),

  getAssessment: () =>
    request("/assessment"),

  submitAssessment: (payload) =>
    request("/assessment/submit", {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    }),
};

export const courseService = {

    request("/courses"),

  getAllCourses: () =>
    request("/courses/all"),

  getCourse: (id) =>
    request(`/courses/${id}`),

  enroll: (courseId, userId) =>
    request(
      `/courses/${courseId}/enroll`,
      {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          user_id: userId,
          lesson_id: lessonId,

      }
    ),

    courseId,
    userId,
    lessonId

      `/courses/${courseId}/lessons/${lessonId}/complete`,
      {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          user_id: userId,
          lesson_id: lessonId,
        }),
      }
    ),
  submitTest: (courseId, payload) =>
    request(
      `/courses/${courseId}/test`,
      {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      }
    ),
};

      `${API_URL}/certificates/${certificateId}/pdf`
    );

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(
        "Não foi possível gerar o certificado."
      );
    }

    return response.blob();
  },

  getAll: () =>
    request("/admin/certificates"),
};

from pathlib import Path
import json
import hashlib
import secrets
from datetime import datetime

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from pydantic import BaseModel


BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data"
DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


app = FastAPI(
    title="SICSI III API",
    version="1.0.0",
    description="API da plataforma SICSI III",
)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=[
        "http://localhost:5173",
        "http://localhost:5174",
        "http://127.0.0.1:5173",
        "http://127.0.0.1:5174",
    ],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)
FILES = {
    "users": DATA_DIR / "users.json",
    "courses": DATA_DIR / "courses.json",
    "assessments": DATA_DIR / "assessments.json",
    "simulations": DATA_DIR / "simulations.json",
    "certificates": DATA_DIR / "certificates.json",
}


def read_json(name):
    path = FILES[name]

    if not path.exists():
        path.write_text("[]", encoding="utf-8")

    try:
        return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    except Exception:
        return []


def write_json(name, data):
    FILES[name].write_text(
        json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )


def password_hash(password):
    return hashlib.pbkdf2_hmac(
        "sha256",
        password.encode(),
        b"sicsi-iii",
        120000,
    ).hex()


def make_id(prefix):
    return f"{prefix}_{secrets.token_hex(6)}"


def now():
    return datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")


class RegisterBody(BaseModel):
    name: str
    email: str
    password: str


class LoginBody(BaseModel):
    email: str
    password: str


class CourseBody(BaseModel):
    title: str
    description: str = ""
    published: bool = True


class LessonBody(BaseModel):
    title: str
    video_url: str


class QuestionBody(BaseModel):
    question: str
    options: list[str]
    correct_answer: int
    explanation: str = ""


class EnrollmentBody(BaseModel):
    user_id: str


class CompletionBody(BaseModel):
    user_id: str
    lesson_id: str


class TestBody(BaseModel):
    user_id: str
    answers: list[str]


class PromoteBody(BaseModel):
    role: str


# ============================================================
# FORÇAR UTF-8 NAS RESPOSTAS HTTP
# ============================================================

@app.middleware("http")
async def force_utf8_response(request, call_next):
    response = await call_next(request)

    content_type = response.headers.get("content-type", "")

    if content_type.startswith("application/json") and "charset=" not in content_type.lower():
        response.headers["content-type"] = "application/json; charset=utf-8"

    elif content_type.startswith("text/") and "charset=" not in content_type.lower():
        response.headers["content-type"] = f"{content_type}; charset=utf-8"

    return response

@app.get("/")
def root():
    return {
        "name": "SICSI III API",
        "status": "online",
        "version": "1.0.0",
    }


@app.get("/api/health")
def health():
    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "service": "sicsi-backend",
    }


@app.get("/api/data/status")
def data_status():
    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "files": {
            key: path.exists()
            for key, path in FILES.items()
        },
    }


# ============================================================
# AUTH
# ============================================================

@app.post("/api/auth/register")
def register(body: RegisterBody):
    users = read_json("users")

    email = body.email.strip().lower()

    if any(u["email"].lower() == email for u in users):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Este email já está registado.",
        )

    user = {
        "id": make_id("usr"),
        "name": body.name.strip(),
        "email": email,
        "password_hash": password_hash(body.password),
        "role": "student",
        "initial_assessment_completed": False,
        "awareness_level": None,
        "assessment_score": None,
        "recommendations": [],
        "enrolled_courses": [],
        "completed_lessons": {},
        "completed_courses": [],
        "simulation_results": {},
        "created_at": now(),
    }

    users.append(user)
    write_json("users", users)

    safe = {
        k: v
        for k, v in user.items()
        if k != "password_hash"
    }

    return {"user": safe}


@app.post("/api/auth/login")
def login(body: LoginBody):
    users = read_json("users")

    email = body.email.strip().lower()

    user = next(
        (
            u for u in users
            if u["email"].lower() == email
        ),
        None,
    )

    if not user or user.get("password_hash") != password_hash(body.password):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=401,
            detail="Email ou palavra-passe incorrectos.",
        )

    safe = {
        k: v
        for k, v in user.items()
        if k != "password_hash"
    }

    return {
        "user": safe,
        "token": secrets.token_urlsafe(24),
    }


@app.get("/api/users")
def get_users():
    users = read_json("users")

    return [
        {
            k: v
            for k, v in user.items()
            if k != "password_hash"
        }
        for user in users
    ]


@app.patch("/api/users/{user_id}/role")
def change_role(user_id: str, body: PromoteBody):
    if body.role not in ["student", "admin"]:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Função inválida.",
        )

    users = read_json("users")

    user = next(
        (u for u in users if u["id"] == user_id),
        None,
    )

    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Utilizador não encontrado.",
        )

    user["role"] = body.role

    write_json("users", users)

    return {
        k: v
        for k, v in user.items()
        if k != "password_hash"
    }

def course_public(course):
    lessons = course.get("lessons", [])
    questions = course.get("questions", [])

    return {
        **course,
        "lessons_count": len(lessons),
        "questions_count": len(questions),
    }


@app.get("/api/courses")
def get_courses():
    courses = read_json("courses")

    return [
        course_public(c)
        for c in courses
        if c.get("published", True)
    ]


@app.get("/api/admin/courses")
def admin_courses():
    return [
        course_public(c)
        for c in read_json("courses")
    ]


@app.get("/api/courses/{course_id}")
def get_course(course_id: str):
    courses = read_json("courses")

    course = next(
        (c for c in courses if c["id"] == course_id),
        None,
    )

    if not course:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Curso não encontrado.",
        )

    return course_public(course)


@app.post("/api/admin/courses")
def create_course(body: CourseBody):
    courses = read_json("courses")

    course = {
        "id": make_id("course"),
        "title": body.title.strip(),
        "description": body.description.strip(),
        "published": body.published,
        "lessons": [],
        "questions": [],
        "created_at": now(),
        "updated_at": now(),
    }

    courses.append(course)
    write_json("courses", courses)

    return course_public(course)


@app.put("/api/admin/courses/{course_id}")
def update_course(course_id: str, body: CourseBody):
    courses = read_json("courses")

    course = next(
        (c for c in courses if c["id"] == course_id),
        None,
    )

    if not course:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Curso não encontrado.",
        )

    course["title"] = body.title.strip()
    course["description"] = body.description.strip()
    course["published"] = body.published
    course["updated_at"] = now()

    write_json("courses", courses)

    return course_public(course)


@app.delete("/api/admin/courses/{course_id}")
def delete_course(course_id: str):
    courses = read_json("courses")

    original = len(courses)

    courses = [
        c for c in courses
        if c["id"] != course_id
    ]

    if len(courses) == original:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Curso não encontrado.",
        )

    write_json("courses", courses)

    return {"status": "deleted"}

@app.post("/api/admin/courses/{course_id}/lessons")
def add_lesson(course_id: str, body: LessonBody):
    courses = read_json("courses")

    course = next(
        (c for c in courses if c["id"] == course_id),
        None,
    )

    if not course:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Curso não encontrado.",
        )

    lesson = {
        "id": make_id("lesson"),
        "title": body.title.strip(),
        "video_url": body.video_url.strip(),
        "order": len(course["lessons"]) + 1,
    }

    course["lessons"].append(lesson)
    course["updated_at"] = now()

    write_json("courses", courses)

    return lesson


@app.put("/api/admin/courses/{course_id}/lessons/{lesson_id}")
def update_lesson(
    course_id: str,
    lesson_id: str,
    body: LessonBody,
):
    courses = read_json("courses")

    course = next(
        (c for c in courses if c["id"] == course_id),
        None,
    )

    if not course:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Curso não encontrado.",
        )

    lesson = next(
        (
            l for l in course["lessons"]
            if l["id"] == lesson_id
        ),
        None,
    )

    if not lesson:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Aula não encontrada.",
        )

    lesson["title"] = body.title.strip()
    lesson["video_url"] = body.video_url.strip()

    write_json("courses", courses)

    return lesson


@app.delete("/api/admin/courses/{course_id}/lessons/{lesson_id}")
def delete_lesson(course_id: str, lesson_id: str):
    courses = read_json("courses")

    course = next(
        (c for c in courses if c["id"] == course_id),
        None,
    )

    if not course:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Curso não encontrado.",
        )

    course["lessons"] = [
        l for l in course["lessons"]
        if l["id"] != lesson_id
    ]

    for index, lesson in enumerate(
        course["lessons"],
        start=1,
    ):
        lesson["order"] = index

    write_json("courses", courses)

    return {"status": "deleted"}


# ============================================================
# QUESTIONS
# ============================================================

@app.post("/api/admin/courses/{course_id}/questions")
def add_question(course_id: str, body: QuestionBody):
    courses = read_json("courses")

    course = next(
        (c for c in courses if c["id"] == course_id),
        None,
    )

    if not course:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Curso não encontrado.",
        )

    if len(body.options) < 2:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="A pergunta precisa de pelo menos duas opções.",
        )

    if body.correct_answer < 0 or body.correct_answer >= len(body.options):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Resposta correcta inválida.",
        )

    question = {
        "id": make_id("question"),
        "question": body.question.strip(),
        "options": body.options,
        "correct_answer": body.correct_answer,
        "explanation": body.explanation.strip(),
    }

    course["questions"].append(question)

    write_json("courses", courses)

    return question


@app.put("/api/admin/courses/{course_id}/questions/{question_id}")
def update_question(
    course_id: str,
    question_id: str,
    body: QuestionBody,
):
    courses = read_json("courses")

    course = next(
        (c for c in courses if c["id"] == course_id),
        None,
    )

    if not course:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Curso não encontrado.",
        )

    question = next(
        (
            q for q in course["questions"]
            if q["id"] == question_id
        ),
        None,
    )

    if not question:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Pergunta não encontrada.",
        )

    question.update({
        "question": body.question.strip(),
        "options": body.options,
        "correct_answer": body.correct_answer,
        "explanation": body.explanation.strip(),
    })

    write_json("courses", courses)

    return question


@app.delete("/api/admin/courses/{course_id}/questions/{question_id}")
def delete_question(course_id: str, question_id: str):
    courses = read_json("courses")

    course = next(
        (c for c in courses if c["id"] == course_id),
        None,
    )

    if not course:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Curso não encontrado.",
        )

    course["questions"] = [
        q for q in course["questions"]
        if q["id"] != question_id
    ]

    write_json("courses", courses)

    return {"status": "deleted"}


# ============================================================
# STUDENT LEARNING
# ============================================================

@app.post("/api/courses/{course_id}/enroll")
def enroll(course_id: str, body: EnrollmentBody):
    users = read_json("users")
    courses = read_json("courses")

    user = next(
        (u for u in users if u["id"] == body.user_id),
        None,
    )

    course = next(
        (c for c in courses if c["id"] == course_id),
        None,
    )

    if not user or not course:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Utilizador ou curso não encontrado.",
        )

    if course_id not in user["enrolled_courses"]:
        user["enrolled_courses"].append(course_id)

    write_json("users", users)

    return {
        "user": {
            k: v
            for k, v in user.items()
            if k != "password_hash"
        }
    }


@app.post("/api/courses/{course_id}/lessons/{lesson_id}/complete")
def complete_lesson(
    course_id: str,
    lesson_id: str,
    body: CompletionBody,
):
    users = read_json("users")
    courses = read_json("courses")

    user = next(
        (u for u in users if u["id"] == body.user_id),
        None,
    )

    course = next(
        (c for c in courses if c["id"] == course_id),
        None,
    )

    if not user or not course:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Dados não encontrados.",
        )

    completed = user["completed_lessons"].setdefault(
        course_id,
        [],
    )

    if lesson_id not in completed:
        completed.append(lesson_id)

    if (
        len(completed) == len(course["lessons"])
        and course_id not in user["completed_courses"]
    ):
        user["completed_courses"].append(course_id)

    write_json("users", users)

    return {
        "completed_lessons": completed,
        "course_completed":
            course_id in user["completed_courses"],
    }


@app.post("/api/courses/{course_id}/test")
def submit_test(course_id: str, body: TestBody):
    users = read_json("users")
    courses = read_json("courses")
    certificates = read_json("certificates")

    user = next(
        (u for u in users if u["id"] == body.user_id),
        None,
    )

    course = next(
        (c for c in courses if c["id"] == course_id),
        None,
    )

    if not user or not course:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Dados não encontrados.",
        )

    questions = course.get("questions", [])

    if len(questions) != 10:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="O curso precisa ter exactamente 10 perguntas no teste final.",
        )

    score = 0
    feedback = []
    submitted_answers = []

    for index, question in enumerate(questions):
        answer = (
            body.answers[index]
            if index < len(body.answers)
            else ""
        )

        answer = str(answer)
        correct_answer = str(question["correct_answer"])
        correct = answer == correct_answer

        if correct:
            score += 1

        submitted_answers.append(answer)

        feedback.append({
            "question": question["question"],
            "correct": correct,
            "explanation": question["explanation"],
        })

    percentage = round((score / len(questions)) * 100)
    passed = percentage >= 70

    user.setdefault("test_results", {})
    user["test_results"].setdefault(
        course_id,
        {
            "attempts": [],
            "last_result": None,
        },
    )

    result = {
        "score": score,
        "total_questions": len(questions),
        "percentage": percentage,
        "passed": passed,
        "answers": submitted_answers,
        "feedback": feedback,
        "submitted_at": now(),
    }

    user["test_results"][course_id]["attempts"].append(result)
    user["test_results"][course_id]["last_result"] = result

    certificate = None

    if passed:
        if course_id not in user.setdefault("completed_courses", []):
            user["completed_courses"].append(course_id)

        existing = next(
            (
                c for c in certificates
                if c["user_id"] == user["id"]
                and c["course_id"] == course_id
            ),
            None,
        )

        if not existing:
            certificate = {
                "id": make_id("cert"),
                "user_id": user["id"],
                "course_id": course_id,
                "student_name": user["name"],
                "course_title": course["title"],
                "score": percentage,
                "percentage": percentage,
                "certificate_code": make_id("SICSI"),
                "issued_at": now(),
            }

            certificates.append(certificate)
        else:
            certificate = existing

    write_json("users", users)
    write_json("certificates", certificates)

    return {
        "score": score,
        "total_questions": len(questions),
        "percentage": percentage,
        "passed": passed,
        "feedback": feedback,
        "certificate": certificate,
        "attempts": user["test_results"][course_id]["attempts"],
        "last_result": user["test_results"][course_id]["last_result"],
    }

# TEST RESULTADOS E CERTIFICADOS

@app.get("/api/courses/{course_id}/test/{user_id}")
def get_test_result(course_id: str, user_id: str):
    users = read_json("users")

    user = next(
        (u for u in users if u["id"] == user_id),
        None,
    )

    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Utilizador não encontrado.",
        )

    test_results = user.get("test_results", {})
    course_result = test_results.get(course_id)

    if not course_result:
        return {
            "has_result": False,
            "attempts": [],
            "last_result": None,
        }

    return {
        "has_result": True,
        "attempts": course_result.get("attempts", []),
        "last_result": course_result.get("last_result"),
    }


@app.get("/api/certificates/{certificate_id}")
def get_certificate(certificate_id: str):
    certificates = read_json("certificates")

    certificate = next(
        (
            c for c in certificates
            if c["id"] == certificate_id
        ),
        None,
    )

    if not certificate:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Certificado não encontrado.",
        )

    return certificate


@app.get("/api/certificates/{certificate_id}/pdf")
def certificate_pdf(certificate_id: str):
    certificates = read_json("certificates")

    certificate = next(
        (
            c for c in certificates
            if c["id"] == certificate_id
        ),
        None,
    )

    if not certificate:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Certificado não encontrado.",
        )

    try:
        from io import BytesIO
        from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4, landscape
        from reportlab.lib import colors
        from reportlab.lib.enums import TA_CENTER
        from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet, ParagraphStyle
        from reportlab.lib.units import cm
        from reportlab.pdfgen import canvas

        buffer = BytesIO()

        page_width, page_height = landscape(A4)

        pdf = canvas.Canvas(
            buffer,
            pagesize=(page_width, page_height),
        )

        # Fundo
        pdf.setFillColor(colors.HexColor("#f8fafc"))
        pdf.rect(
            0,
            0,
            page_width,
            page_height,
            fill=1,
            stroke=0,
        )

        # Moldura exterior
        pdf.setStrokeColor(colors.HexColor("#2563eb"))
        pdf.setLineWidth(3)
        pdf.rect(
            1.2 * cm,
            1.2 * cm,
            page_width - 2.4 * cm,
            page_height - 2.4 * cm,
            fill=0,
            stroke=1,
        )

        # Moldura interior
        pdf.setStrokeColor(colors.HexColor("#bfdbfe"))
        pdf.setLineWidth(1)
        pdf.rect(
            1.65 * cm,
            1.65 * cm,
            page_width - 3.3 * cm,
            page_height - 3.3 * cm,
            fill=0,
            stroke=1,
        )

        center_x = page_width / 2

        pdf.setFillColor(colors.HexColor("#1d4ed8"))
        pdf.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 28)
        pdf.drawCentredString(
            center_x,
            page_height - 3.2 * cm,
            "SICSI III",
        )

        pdf.setFillColor(colors.HexColor("#64748b"))
        pdf.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 11)
        pdf.drawCentredString(
            center_x,
            page_height - 4.1 * cm,
            "CERTIFICADO DE CONCLUSÃO",
        )

        pdf.setFillColor(colors.HexColor("#0f172a"))
        pdf.setFont("Helvetica", 12)
        pdf.drawCentredString(
            center_x,
            page_height - 5.6 * cm,
            "Certificamos que",
        )

        pdf.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 25)
        pdf.drawCentredString(
            center_x,
            page_height - 6.8 * cm,
            certificate["student_name"],
        )

        pdf.setFont("Helvetica", 12)
        pdf.setFillColor(colors.HexColor("#475569"))
        pdf.drawCentredString(
            center_x,
            page_height - 8.0 * cm,
            "concluiu com aproveitamento o curso",
        )

        pdf.setFillColor(colors.HexColor("#0f172a"))
        pdf.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 19)
        pdf.drawCentredString(
            center_x,
            page_height - 9.2 * cm,
            certificate["course_title"],
        )

        pdf.setFillColor(colors.HexColor("#475569"))
        pdf.setFont("Helvetica", 10)
        pdf.drawCentredString(
            center_x,
            page_height - 10.4 * cm,
            "no âmbito da plataforma de consciencialização em segurança digital SICSI III.",
        )

        # Resultado
        pdf.setFillColor(colors.HexColor("#eff6ff"))
        pdf.roundRect(
            center_x - 3.2 * cm,
            page_height - 13.0 * cm,
            6.4 * cm,
            1.4 * cm,
            0.25 * cm,
            fill=1,
            stroke=0,
        )

        pdf.setFillColor(colors.HexColor("#1d4ed8"))
        pdf.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 18)
        pdf.drawCentredString(
            center_x,
            page_height - 12.1 * cm,
            f"Aproveitamento: {certificate.get('percentage', certificate.get('score', 0))}%",
        )

        # Código
        pdf.setFillColor(colors.HexColor("#64748b"))
        pdf.setFont("Helvetica", 9)
        pdf.drawCentredString(
            center_x,
            2.8 * cm,
            f"Código de validação: {certificate.get('certificate_code', certificate['id'])}",
        )

        pdf.drawCentredString(
            center_x,
            2.25 * cm,
            f"Emitido em: {certificate.get('issued_at', '')}",
        )

        pdf.setFillColor(colors.HexColor("#1d4ed8"))
        pdf.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 9)
        pdf.drawCentredString(
            center_x,
            1.7 * cm,
            "SICSI III · Segurança Digital",
        )

        pdf.showPage()
        pdf.save()

        buffer.seek(0)

        filename = (
            certificate.get("certificate_code", certificate["id"])
            + ".pdf"
        )

        return StreamingResponse(
            buffer,
            media_type="application/pdf",
            headers={
                "Content-Disposition": f'inline; filename="{filename}"'
            },
        )

    except ImportError:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=500,
            detail="A biblioteca necessária para gerar PDF não está instalada.",
        )

@app.get("/api/admin/stats")
def admin_stats():
    users = read_json("users")
    courses = read_json("courses")
    certificates = read_json("certificates")

    return {
        "users": len(users),
        "students": len(
            [u for u in users if u.get("role") == "student"]
        ),
        "admins": len(
            [u for u in users if u.get("role") == "admin"]
        ),
        "courses": len(courses),
        "published_courses": len(
            [c for c in courses if c.get("published", True)]
        ),
        "certificates": len(certificates),
    }

# BOOTSTRAP ADMIN LOCAL
# ============================================================

def ensure_admin():
    users = read_json("users")

    existing_admin = next(
        (u for u in users if u.get("email", "").lower() == "admin@sicsi.local"),
        None,
    )

    if existing_admin:
        existing_admin["role"] = "admin"
        existing_admin["password_hash"] = password_hash("Admin@123")
        write_json("users", users)
        return

    admin = {
        "id": "usr_admin",
        "name": "Administrador SICSI",
        "email": "admin@sicsi.local",
        "password_hash": password_hash("Admin@123"),
        "role": "admin",
        "initial_assessment_completed": True,
        "awareness_level": "Administrador",
        "assessment_score": 100,
        "recommendations": [],
        "enrolled_courses": [],
        "completed_lessons": {},
        "completed_courses": [],
        "simulation_results": {},
        "created_at": now(),
    }

    write_json("users", [admin])


ensure_admin()

@app.get("/api/assessment/questions")
def assessment_questions():
    return [
        {
            "id": q["id"],
            "question": q["question"],
            "options": q["options"],
        }
        for q in ASSESSMENT_QUESTIONS
    ]


@app.post("/api/assessment/submit")
def submit_assessment(body: TestBody):
    users = read_json("users")

    user = next(
        (u for u in users if u["id"] == body.user_id),
        None,
    )

    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Estudante não encontrado.",
        )

    score = 0

    for index, question in enumerate(ASSESSMENT_QUESTIONS):
        answer = (
            body.answers[index]
            if index < len(body.answers)
            else -1
        )

        if answer == question["correct"]:
            score += 1

    percentage = score * 10

    if percentage < 40:
        level = "Iniciante"
        recommendations = [
            "Fundamentos de Segurança Digital",
            "Protecção de Contas e Credenciais",
            "Reconhecimento de Phishing",
        ]
    elif percentage < 70:
        level = "Intermédio"
        recommendations = [
            "Reconhecimento de Phishing",
            "Engenharia Social",
            "Fraudes Digitais",
        ]
    else:
        level = "Avançado"
        recommendations = [
            "Phishing Avançado",
            "Engenharia Social",
            "Fraudes Digitais",
        ]

    user["initial_assessment_completed"] = True
    user["assessment_score"] = percentage
    user["awareness_level"] = level
    user["recommendations"] = recommendations

    write_json("users", users)

    return {
        "score": score,
        "percentage": percentage,
        "level": level,
        "recommendations": recommendations,
    }

@app.get("/api/simulations")
def get_simulations():
    return read_json("simulations")


@app.get("/api/simulations/{simulation_id}")
def get_simulation(simulation_id: str):
    simulations = read_json("simulations")

    simulation = next(
        (
            item
            for item in simulations
            if item.get("id") == simulation_id
        ),
        None,
    )

    if not simulation:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Simulação não encontrada.",
        )

    return simulation


@app.post("/api/simulations/{simulation_id}/evaluate")
def evaluate_simulation(
    simulation_id: str,
    body: dict,
):
    simulations = read_json("simulations")

    simulation = next(
        (
            item
            for item in simulations
            if item.get("id") == simulation_id
        ),
        None,
    )

    if not simulation:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=404,
            detail="Simulação não encontrada.",
        )

    action = body.get("action")
    user_id = body.get("user_id")

    if not action:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="É necessário indicar uma ação.",
        )

    safe_action = simulation.get("safe_action")

    if action == safe_action:
        correct = True

        feedback = (
            "Boa decisão. Esta é a resposta mais segura "
            "para esta situação."
        )

        explanation = simulation.get(
            "explanation",
            "A situação foi analisada correctamente.",
        )
    else:
        correct = False

        feedback = (
            "Atenção. Esta ação pode expor os seus dados "
            "ou aumentar o risco de segurança."
        )

        explanation = simulation.get(
            "explanation",
            "Antes de agir, analise cuidadosamente os sinais de risco.",
        )

    if user_id:
        users = read_json("users")

        user = next(
            (
                item
                for item in users
                if item.get("id") == user_id
            ),
            None,
        )

        if user:
            results = user.setdefault(
                "simulation_results",
                {},
            )

            results[simulation_id] = {
                "action": action,
                "correct": correct,
                "completed": correct,
                "updated_at": now(),
            }

            write_json("users", users)

    return {
        "correct": correct,
        "feedback": feedback,
        "explanation": explanation,
        "safe_action": safe_action,
        "completed": correct,
    }
